# Product test matrix

Generated by `npm run matrix` from three sources: the product catalog and execution templates, the scenario
specs in `test/matrix/specs/`, and the results the test runs wrote to `test/matrix/results/`. The same content as
data is in `docs/test-matrix.json`. How the harness works, and how to add a family, is in `test/matrix/README.md`.

**How to read it**

- Every catalog product and every execution template has a row. A product with no scenario is **not yet specified**.
- A scenario is a list of steps on one fictional instrument of exactly that product, in its own disposable database.
  After every step the cash buckets, positions, pending obligations, P&L, net asset value, balance sheet,
  borrowings and new history rows must equal figures worked out by hand in the spec, and the integrity check must be
  clean. At the end the application is restarted and everything is read again.
- The same scenario runs at three levels: **Engine** (in-process, `npm test`), **API** (HTTP against a disposable
  server, `npm run test:api`) and **Browser** (the interface in Chromium against a disposable server, with every
  Accounting screen read back and compared with both the API and the spec, `npm run test:browser`).
- Test ids are `E:<product>:<step>`, `A:<product>:<step>` and `B:<product>:<step>` (`E:T:<template>:<step>` for a
  template). The tables give the step names; put the level letter and the product id in front.
- Statuses come only from the results files. **passed**: the step ran and every figure matched. **blocked** and
  **unsupported**: the spec declares that the Terminal does not allow the action (blocked: not in this state;
  unsupported: not at all, it is recorded by hand instead), and the test asserted that the Terminal refused it
  visibly, with the stated reason, and that nothing changed. **failed**: a figure did not match; the scenario stops
  there. **not run**: no result at that level, because the level was not run for the product, an earlier step failed,
  or the spec changed after the last run. Nothing is counted as tested because another product shares its engine.

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `common_stock` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | cash dividend (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand; stock split (ratio, ex-date), recorded by hand | T+1 on the US equity calendar (Book setting settlement.equity = 1). Cash dividend paid on its date from the holding at the open of the ex-date; split applied on its ex-date (quantity changes, cost does not) | Average cost; commission expensed; payable or receivable until settlement; realized P&L on sale; dividend income; valued at last price. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `preferred_stock` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | preferred dividend (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand | T+1 on the US equity calendar. Quarterly preferred dividend from the recorded corporate action, paid per position from the holding at the open of the ex-date | Average cost per position (two ticket purchases are two positions); commission in basis points of principal; dividend income. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `adr` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | cash dividend (ex-date, amount per ADR), recorded by hand; depositary fee, recorded by hand as a manual cash flow of kind Fee on the position | T+1 on the US equity calendar: the listing venue decides, not the issuer domicile. Cash dividend net of the Book withholding assumption (15%); depositary fee by hand | Average cost; dividend income gross with withholding booked as a fee; depositary fee booked as a fee. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `gdr` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | cash dividend (ex-date, amount per GDR), recorded by hand; depositary fee, recorded by hand as a manual cash flow of kind Fee on the position | T+2 on the London calendar (Book setting settlement.foreignCash = 2); Good Friday and Easter Monday are skipped. Cash dividend from the recorded corporate action; depositary fee by hand | Average cost; commission in basis points with a minimum per order; dividend income; depositary fee booked as a fee. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `etf` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares; Order type and Limit price for the limit order; Sell from (when the Account holds the fund in more than one position) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | cash distribution (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand | T+1 on the US equity calendar. A working limit order fills on a later engine cycle once the ask reaches the limit; cash distribution per position from the recorded corporate action | Nothing is booked while an order only works; average cost per position; sales reduce the position named on the ticket. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `etn` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares; State a fill price (for the recovery recorded by hand) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | manual price after quotes stop (Instrument drawer, Overview, Enter a price by hand); issuer credit event: final recovery recorded as a sale at a stated fill price | T+1 on the US equity calendar. No scheduled events. Issuer redemption and credit events are not simulated; recorded by hand | Average cost; a position with no price is carried at cost and the net asset value is marked provisional; a manual price is labelled manual; a stated-price fill is recorded as a manual input. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `closed_end_fund` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Shares | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | income distribution (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand; return of capital, recorded by hand as a manual cash flow of kind Return of capital on the position | T+1 on the US equity calendar, per fill. An order larger than the displayed size fills in parts, one per engine cycle; income distribution from the recorded corporate action; return of capital by hand | Average cost over the partial fills; commission by the fee schedule on the order, charged as it fills; return of capital reduces cost and is not income. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `reit` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Units | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; USD/JPY rate (FX fixture) for reporting-currency figures | cash distribution (ex-date, yen per unit), recorded by hand | T+2 on the Tokyo calendar (settlement.foreignCash = 2); the Vernal Equinox holiday on 20 March 2026 is skipped; cash settles in yen. Cash distribution in yen from the recorded corporate action | Average cost in yen; every entry also stored in US dollars at the rate when posted; balances translated at the current rate, the difference reported as FX effect. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `warrant` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket) with "State a fill price" for the events recorded by hand. Required: Account; Action; Shares; State a fill price (for exercise, conversion and lapse recorded by hand) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | exercise: sale of the warrants at their average cost and purchase of the shares at strike plus warrant cost, both at stated prices; lapse: sale of the remaining warrants at a stated price of zero | T+1 on the US equity calendar; a sale at zero has nothing to settle. None simulated. Exercise and expiry are recorded by hand; an automatic exercise is refused | Average cost; on exercise the warrant cost moves into the cost of the shares with no profit or loss; a lapse realizes the remaining cost as a loss. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `subscription_right` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket) with "State a fill price" for the events recorded by hand. Required: Account; Action; Shares; State a fill price (for exercise, conversion and lapse recorded by hand); Sell from (two positions are held) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | exercise: sale of the rights at their average cost and purchase of the new shares at subscription price plus rights cost, both at stated prices; lapse: sale of the unexercised rights at a stated price of zero | T+1 on the US equity calendar; a sale at zero has nothing to settle. None simulated. Exercise and lapse are recorded by hand; an automatic exercise is refused | Average cost per position; on exercise the rights cost moves into the cost of the new shares; a lapse realizes the remaining cost as a loss. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `convertible_preferred` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket) with "State a fill price" for the events recorded by hand. Required: Account; Action; Shares; State a fill price (for exercise, conversion and lapse recorded by hand) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | preferred dividend (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand; conversion: sale of the preferred at its average cost and purchase of the common shares at the cost carried over, both at stated prices | T+1 on the US equity calendar; the two conversion tickets settle on the same day and net to no cash. Quarterly preferred dividend from the recorded corporate action. Conversion is not simulated: recorded by hand; an automatic conversion is refused | Average cost; on conversion the cost of the preferred becomes the cost of the common shares with no profit or loss and no net cash. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `short_sale` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: Sell short (adds the securities borrow leg), then Buy to cover; the strategy instance for Close. Required: Account; Action (Sell short / Buy to cover); Shares; borrow assumption only when no borrow data is supplied | bid, ask, last (quote fixture); borrow availability and fee (borrow fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); collateral and margin from the Book short assumptions; settlement date; commission | dividend on the borrowed shares (ex-date, amount), recorded by hand | T+1 on the US equity calendar; sale proceeds settle into restricted cash; a cover is paid from restricted cash first. Daily borrow fee accrual; end-of-day collateral mark and margin reserve; dividend compensation paid to the lender; fee paid when the borrow is fully returned (otherwise monthly) | Short position at negative cost (proceeds); unrealized against last price; borrow fee and dividend compensation under borrowing and funding expenses; the borrow is one record in the borrowing register. Collateral: Restricted cash = 102% of market value, marked daily; 30% margin reserve; neither is buying power or withdrawable |

## Fund (NAV-priced) (`fund`)

2 products, 2 specified in `test/matrix/specs/fund.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Mutual fund (`mutual_fund`) | Lakemont Balanced Fund, Investor Class: US open-ended mutual fund dealt at the next NAV | 27 | complete, 0.6 s | complete, 1.7 s | complete, 25.6 s |
| Money-market fund (`money_market_fund`) | Harborline Treasury Money Market Fund, Institutional Shares: constant NAV 1.00, T+0, held by Treasury | 16 | complete, 0.4 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 17.7 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mutual_fund` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy subscribes, Sell redeems), then the trade preview. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Shares | dealing date from the order time, the 16:00 New York cut-off and the fund dealing calendar; NAV of the dealing date (close fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); last published NAV for the estimate and for valuation (price fixture); settlement date counted from the dealing date; commission from the Book fee schedule (none for a no-load class) | income distribution (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand as a cash dividend | T+1 from the dealing date on the US calendar (Book setting settlement.fund = 1); the dealing date is the day of the order before 16:00 New York, else the next dealing day. Orders wait for the NAV of their dealing date (never filled at a live quote, never expired at the end of the day); distribution paid on its ex-date; NAV drops by the distribution | Average cost; subscription cost reserved while waiting; payable or receivable until settlement; realized P&L on redemption; distribution income; valued at the latest published NAV. Collateral: None; limit orders and short sales do not apply to a fund and are refused |
| `money_market_fund` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy subscribes, Sell redeems), then the trade preview. Required: Account (trading owner: Treasury); Action; Shares | dealing date from the order time, the 16:00 New York cut-off and the fund dealing calendar; NAV of the dealing date (close fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); last published NAV for the estimate and for valuation (price fixture); settlement date counted from the dealing date; commission from the Book fee schedule (none for a no-load class) | monthly dividend (ex-date, amount per share), recorded by hand as a cash distribution; the daily accrual is the fund's own and is not simulated | Same day (T+0, the instrument's settlement lag) from the dealing date, on the US calendar. Subscription and redemptions wait for the NAV of their dealing date; a redemption after the cut-off deals the next dealing day; monthly dividend paid on its ex-date | Average cost at 1.00; reserved subscription cash not transferable; no realized P&L at a constant NAV; dividend income in Treasury. Collateral: None |

Fund shares are subscribed and redeemed with the fund; they are not borrowed and sold short. |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `private_equity` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket; Buy and Sell carry the product's own labels), always with "State a fill price"; then the trade preview. Events by hand: the position's Lifecycle menu in the strategy instance. Required: Trading owner; Action (Commit or acquire, Sell (secondary)); Share of the interest; State a fill price; Commitment (registration); Valuation goes stale after (registration) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the instrument's calendar (or a date stated on the ticket); cost, realized P&L and the manual workflow figures worked out from what is entered | commitment and fund terms at registration; capital calls (amount, management-fee part); valuations (value of the holding, valuation date, source); distributions (return of capital, realized gain, income, carried interest withheld); secondary sale price and transfer date; final wind-up | Capital calls and distributions in cash when recorded; the secondary sale on the transfer date stated on the ticket (13 November 2026). Nothing automated. Calls, valuations, distributions and the wind-up recorded by hand to completion; a call above the uncalled commitment is refused | Cost = investment part of calls less return of capital; fee parts and carried interest in Fees; gains in Realized P&L; income in Dividends; valued at the GP valuation rolled forward for later calls and distributions; stale after 120 days (NAV provisional). Collateral: None: a fund interest held long, no counterparty exposure beyond the fund itself |
| `venture_capital` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket; Buy and Sell carry the product's own labels), always with "State a fill price"; then the trade preview. Events by hand: the position's Lifecycle menu in the strategy instance. Required: Trading owner; Action (Commit or acquire); Share of the interest; State a fill price; Commitment (registration); Valuation goes stale after (registration) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the instrument's calendar (or a date stated on the ticket); cost, realized P&L and the manual workflow figures worked out from what is entered | commitment and fund terms at registration; capital calls with their management-fee part; fund expenses billed outside the calls; valuations with date and source; distributions (return of capital, realized gain); final wind-up | Calls, expenses and distributions in EUR cash when recorded; nothing waits for settlement. Nothing automated; calls, valuations, distributions and the wind-up by hand. A redemption notice is not offered (closed-end fund) | In EUR, reported in USD at the fixture rate in force when each entry is posted; FX effect on the EUR balances at the current rate; cost, fees, realized and unrealized as for private equity. Collateral: None: a fund interest held long |
| `hedge_fund` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket; Buy and Sell carry the product's own labels), always with "State a fill price"; then the trade preview. Events by hand: the position's Lifecycle menu in the strategy instance. Required: Trading owner; Action (Subscribe); Shares; State a fill price (the dealing NAV); Dealing frequency, notice, payment days (registration); Valuation goes stale after (registration) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the instrument's calendar (or a date stated on the ticket); cost, realized P&L and the manual workflow figures worked out from what is entered; dealing date from the notice period and the dealing frequency; gate cap and the shares carried to the next dealing date; payment date of redemption proceeds on the payment calendar | subscription NAV (stated fill price); monthly NAV per share with date and source; redemption notice (shares); NAV per share struck for each dealing date | Subscription T+2 on the USD payment calendar; redemption proceeds 10 business days after the dealing date (14 July and 15 October 2026). Redemption only by notice: dealt on the first quarter end at least 45 days after the notice, at most 50% of the shares held at the first dealing date each time; each dealing date waits (blocked, visible) until its NAV is entered by hand. Lock-up refused before 31 March 2026; a sale on the ticket is refused | Average cost per share; realized P&L on each redemption at the dealing NAV; valued at the latest NAV per share, stale after 35 days (NAV provisional). Collateral: None: a fund interest held long |
| `infrastructure` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket; Buy and Sell carry the product's own labels), always with "State a fill price"; then the trade preview. Events by hand: the position's Lifecycle menu in the strategy instance. Required: Trading owner (Treasury); Action (Acquire, Sell); Share of the asset; State a fill price (price for the whole asset); Settlement date (financial close); Asset (registration); Valuation goes stale after (registration) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the instrument's calendar (or a date stated on the ticket); cost, realized P&L and the manual workflow figures worked out from what is entered | purchase and sale prices with their closing dates; valuations (value of the stake, date, source); project distributions (income); capital expenditure; operating expenses | Purchase and sales on the closing dates stated on the ticket (16 March, 16 November, 29 December 2026), on the US calendar. Nothing automated; distributions, capex, expenses and valuations by hand; the valuation goes stale after 190 days | Cost = purchase price plus capital expenditure; distributions in Dividends; expenses in Fees; valued at the appraisal plus capex since; realized P&L on each sale at average cost. Collateral: None: an equity stake held long |
| `direct_real_estate` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket; Buy and Sell carry the product's own labels), always with "State a fill price"; then the trade preview. Events by hand: the position's Lifecycle menu in the strategy instance. Required: Trading owner; Action (Acquire, Sell); Share of the asset; State a fill price (price for the whole asset); Settlement date (completion); Asset (registration); Valuation goes stale after (registration) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the instrument's calendar (or a date stated on the ticket); cost, realized P&L and the manual workflow figures worked out from what is entered | purchase and sale prices with completion dates; acquisition costs and capital expenditure (added to cost); rent received; operating expenses; valuations with date and source | Purchase and sales on the completion dates stated on the ticket, checked against the UK calendar (7 April after Easter; 30 December after Christmas and 28 December). Nothing automated; rent, expenses, capex and appraisals by hand; valuation stale after 180 days | In GBP, reported in USD at 1.25; cost = price + capitalised costs and capex; rent in Dividends; expenses in Fees; valued at the appraisal plus capex since; realized P&L on each sale at average cost. Collateral: None: a building owned outright |
| `insurance_linked_security` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket; Buy and Sell carry the product's own labels), always with "State a fill price"; then the trade preview. Events by hand: the position's Lifecycle menu in the strategy instance. Required: Trading owner; Action (Buy); Face amount; State a fill price (% of face); Peril and maturity (registration); Valuation goes stale after (registration) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the instrument's calendar (or a date stated on the ticket); cost, realized P&L and the manual workflow figures worked out from what is entered | purchase price; coupons received; broker indications as valuations (% of face, date, source); loss event (face written down); redemption price at maturity | Purchase T+2 on the USD payment calendar; redemption T+2 after maturity (31 December 2026 to 5 January 2027, across New Year's Day). Maturity is a lifecycle item that waits, visibly, for the redemption entered by hand; coupons and the loss event by hand; an early redemption is refused | Face amount at average cost in % of face; coupons in Coupon and interest income; principal written down realized as a loss at its cost; valued at the latest broker indication, stale after 45 days. Collateral: None held by the investor: the note is backed by a collateral trust at the issuer (recorded in the terms, not simulated) |
| `collateral_swap` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract); Contracts; State a fill price; Both baskets and their values, fee notional, fee, payer, day count, start and end (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks); fee for each period = fee notional x bp / 10,000 x days / 360 | the baskets and their values (recorded on the contract); marks (value to us, date, source); the end of each fee period | Fees in cash when each period is recorded; nothing else settles: the baskets are not moved by the Terminal. The end date waits, visibly, for the termination entered by hand; the last fee is paid with it | Zero cost; fees in Borrowing and funding expenses (we pay for the upgrade); valued at the mark by hand; the securities exchanged are recorded only (to move them, book the two securities loans instead). Collateral: Position-level terms: fixed independent amount 500,000 posted at entry, returned at termination |
| `freight_contract` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract); Quantity (days); State a fill price; Route, settlement index, quantity unit, contract rate, side, period (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | marks (value to us, date, source); each month's average of the settlement index and the days settled | Each monthly settlement T+2 on the USD payment calendar after it is entered (4 November, 3 December 2026, 6 January 2027). The end of the period waits, visibly, for the last month entered by hand; each month settles its own days and the contract closes with the last | Zero cost; valued at the mark per day by hand; each month realized: (average - contract rate) x days, sign reversed for the seller. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 10% of 15,000 x days open (135,000, then 90,000, 45,000, 0), returned as months settle |
| `credit_spread_forward` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract); Contracts; State a fill price; Reference obligation, forward spread, notional, risky duration, side, expiry (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | risky duration (a contract term, from the desk); marks (value to us, date, source); the spread at the fixing date | Entry T+2 on the US calendar; settlement T+2 after the spread is entered on the fixing date (15 September 2026, paid 17 September). The fixing date waits, visibly, for the spread entered by hand | Zero cost; valued at the mark by hand; settlement = notional x duration x (spread - forward spread) / 10,000; realized. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 5% of notional (500,000) posted at entry, returned at settlement |
| `credit_spread_option` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract, Terminate); Contracts; State a fill price (the premium); Reference obligation, strike, notional, risky duration, payer or receiver, side, expiry (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required): uncollateralized | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | premium; marks (value to us, date, source); agreed price of the half sold back; the spread at expiry | Premium and the half sold T+2 on the US calendar; nothing to settle at expiry when out of the money. Expiry waits, visibly, for the spread entered by hand; payer payoff = notional x duration x max(spread - strike, 0) / 10,000 | Cost = premium; valued at the mark by hand; the half sold realized; the rest expires worthless (premium realized as a loss). Collateral: Uncollateralized, an explicit paper assumption on the contract |
| `variance_swap` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Expiry is a lifecycle item that waits, visibly, for the realised volatility entered by hand; the settlement amount is worked out with the formula and the position closed | Zero cost at entry; valued at the mark entered by hand (negative when against us); termination and final settlement realized; settlement = variance notional x (realised vol² - strike²). Collateral: Position-level terms: variation margin on the marks, threshold 100,000, minimum transfer 25,000; posted from settled cash, received into restricted cash, returned when the contract ends |
| `volatility_swap` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract); Contracts; State a fill price; Strike, vega notional, side, expiry (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required): an agreement of the Book | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | the paper CSA (Treasury, Collateral); marks (value to us, date, source); realised volatility at expiry | Entry T+2 on the USD payment calendar; final settlement T+2 after the realised volatility is entered (21 September 2026, paid 23 September). A contract with no collateral basis cannot be entered (refused); expiry waits, visibly, for the realised volatility entered by hand | Zero cost at entry; valued at the mark entered by hand; final settlement = vega notional x (realised vol - strike), realized. Collateral: Agreement: fixed independent amount 50,000 posted at entry and returned at the end; variation margin on the marks (threshold 0, minimum transfer 10,000) |
| `correlation_swap` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract, Terminate); Contracts; State a fill price; Basket, strike, notional per point, side, expiry (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | marks (value to us, date and a source, required); agreed termination amount; realised correlation at expiry | Entry and termination T+2 on the US calendar; final settlement T+2 after the realised correlation is entered (21 December 2026, paid 23 December). Expiry waits, visibly, for the realised correlation entered by hand; a valuation with no source is refused | Zero cost; valued at the mark by hand; settlement = notional per point x (realised - strike), sign reversed for the seller; realized. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 10% of 2,500,000 posted at entry, trued up on the partial termination, returned at the end; no variation margin |
| `dispersion_swap` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract); Contracts; State a fill price (the upfront); Index, constituents, strike, notional per point, side, expiry (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required): uncollateralized | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | upfront; marks (value to us, date, source); weighted constituent volatility and index volatility at expiry | Upfront T+2 on the US calendar; final settlement T+2 after the realised values are entered (21 September 2026, paid 23 September). Expiry waits, visibly, for the realised values entered by hand; a settlement missing the index volatility is refused | Cost = upfront; valued at the mark by hand; settlement = notional per point x ((constituents - index) - strike); realized against the upfront. Collateral: Uncollateralized, stated on the contract as an explicit paper assumption: nothing posted or received |
| `recovery_swap` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract); Contracts; State a fill price; Reference entity, fixed recovery, notional, side, maturity (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required) | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | marks (value to us, date, source); credit event (date, note); recovery rate from the auction final price | Entry T+2 on the US calendar; settlement T+2 after the recovery rate is entered (14 May 2026, paid 18 May). Settles only after a credit event recorded by hand (refused before); with no credit event by maturity it ends with nothing paid (the maturity waits as a lifecycle item) | Zero cost; valued at the mark by hand; settlement = notional x (recovery - fixed recovery) / 100, sign reversed for the seller; realized. Collateral: Position-level terms: fixed independent amount 300,000 posted at entry and returned at settlement |
| `weather_derivative` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: "Enter the contract" (and "Terminate (agreed amount)") with "State a fill price" (the upfront or the agreed amount per contract); then the trade preview. Marks and the final settlement: the position's Lifecycle menu. Required: Trading owner; Action (Enter the contract, Terminate); Contracts; State a fill price (the premium); Station, index, base temperature, period, strike, tick, cap, payoff, side (registration); Collateral basis (registration, required): uncollateralized | settlement date from the Book's manual lag on the contract's calendar; the settlement amount from the realised value with the contract's formula; collateral under the stated basis (independent amount, variation margin from the marks) | premium; marks (value to us, date, source); agreed price of the part sold back; the HDD total for the period | Premium and the part sold T+2 on the US calendar; final settlement T+2 after the HDD total is entered (1 April 2027, paid 5 April). The end of the period waits, visibly, for the index entered by hand; payout = min(tick x max(HDD - strike, 0), cap) | Cost = premium; valued at the mark by hand; the part sold and the final payout realized against the premium. Collateral: Uncollateralized, an explicit paper assumption on the contract |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `treasury_bill` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account (Treasury); Action; Face amount; Settlement, when a trade must settle sooner than the convention | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | the bank discount rate is converted to a price in % of par by hand (the Terminal has no discount-yield quotation); same-day settlement stated on the ticket for a purchase on the day before maturity | T+1 on the US bond calendar; a trade must settle before the maturity date, so the day before maturity only a same-day settlement stated on the ticket is accepted. No coupon and no accrual; redemption at par on the maturity date, automatic; a trade in the matured bill is refused | Clean cost at average; no accrued interest; discount earned is unrealized P&L while held and realized P&L at sale and at redemption; owned by Treasury, so the figures are Treasury's and the Book's, not an Account's. Collateral: None for a long position; a short sale is refused without securities-borrow data |
| `treasury_note` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none: coupon schedule, accrued interest and settlement follow from the registered terms | T+1 on the US bond calendar (Book setting settlement.bond = 1); Veterans Day is skipped; an order placed on the holiday waits and is matched the next bond-market day. Daily accrual into coupon income; semi-annual coupon paid on the next payment day after a Sunday coupon date to the face settled before it; next coupon and maturity scheduled | Clean cost at average; accrued interest bought and sold through Accrued income; coupon income separate from realized and unrealized P&L; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `treasury_bond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: Sell short (adds the securities borrow leg), then Buy to cover; the Close button of the short position. Required: Account; Action (Sell short / Buy to cover); Face amount; borrow assumption only when no borrow data is supplied | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture); borrow availability and fee (borrow fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); accrued interest received on the short sale and paid on the cover; collateral and margin from the Book short assumptions; settlement date; commission | none | T+1 on the US bond calendar; sale proceeds (principal plus accrued) settle into restricted cash; a cover is paid from restricted cash first. Daily interest cost on the short and daily borrow fee; collateral marked each end of day to 102% of clean value plus accrued; coupon paid to the lender on the next payment day after a holiday coupon date; fee paid when the borrow is returned | Short position at negative clean cost; accrued coupon owed as a negative accrued balance; the coupon paid and the accrued received and paid net to an interest cost in coupon and interest income; borrow fee under borrowing and funding; realized P&L on the covers. Collateral: Restricted cash = 102% of market value including accrued interest, marked daily; 30% margin reserve on the clean value; neither is buying power or withdrawable |
| `tips` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 100) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | Registration: Index ratio (required; no inflation index feed) and Deflation floor; Index ratio recorded by hand on the instrument form (Edit, Lifecycle events recorded by hand: New index ratio, Index ratio as of, Review, Record index ratio) | T+1 on the US bond calendar; principal = face x index ratio x clean price; accrued on the adjusted principal; Christmas skipped. Daily accrual on the adjusted principal; index ratio changes by hand book inflation accretion (income or loss) and true up accrued; final coupon on face x ratio; redemption at face x ratio floored at par (deflation floor) | Cost includes inflation accretion; accretion and coupon in coupon and interest income; realized P&L on the sale and on the floored redemption; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `strips` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount; a stated fill price or a manual price while no quote exists; Order type, Limit price and Time in force for the resting sale | settlement date; commission; bid, ask and last once a quote is supplied (quote fixture) | fill price stated on the ticket for the first purchase (no quote exists); price entered by hand on the instrument, used to value the position and to fill the second purchase with the assumed half spread | T+1 on the US bond calendar; Columbus Day is skipped; a limit order that fills on a later day settles T+1 from the day it fills. No coupon and no accrual; maturity scheduled; without a price the position is carried at cost and the net asset value is provisional | Clean cost at average across a stated-price fill and a manual-mark fill; no accrued interest; realized P&L on each sale; minimum commission. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `foreign_gov_bill` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | the money-market yield is converted to a price in % of par by hand (the Terminal has no yield quotation) | T+1 on the London calendar (venue country GB); UK bank holidays are skipped; a settlement date stated on a UK holiday is refused on the ticket. No coupon and no accrual; redemption at par on the maturity date by the London payment calendar, on a day New York is closed | Sterling position, cash and settlement; US dollar figures at the current rate for balances and unrealized P&L, at the booking rate for commission and realized P&L; the difference is reported as FX effects. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `foreign_gov_bond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none | T+1 on the Tokyo calendar (venue country JP); amounts in whole yen. Daily accrual on ACT/365, on a Tokyo holiday too; semi-annual coupon of exactly half the annual rate, paid on the next Tokyo business day after a Sunday coupon date | Yen position, cash, accrued interest and settlement; US dollar figures at the current rate for balances and unrealized P&L, at the booking rate for income, commission and realized P&L; FX effects reported separately. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `foreign_inflation_linked` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | Registration: Index ratio (required; no inflation index feed), Deflation floor left off (UK index-linked gilts have none); Index ratio recorded by hand on the instrument form (Edit, Lifecycle events recorded by hand) | T+1 on the London calendar (venue country GB); principal = face x index ratio x clean price; English bank holidays skipped; New York holiday does not stop a London trade; ex-dividend period not modelled. Daily accrual on the adjusted principal (New York end of day); index ratio changes book inflation accretion (positive or negative) and true up accrued; coupon on face x ratio; no floor at maturity | Sterling position, cost including accretion, accrued, coupon, sale proceeds; US dollar figures at the current rate for balances and unrealized P&L, at the booking rate for income, commission and realized P&L. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `em_local_debt` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none (local holidays would have to be added by hand in Settings: the calendar is weekends only) | T+3 from the instrument's own settlement convention, counted on weekends only because no South African calendar exists; every preview carries the calendar-fallback warning. Daily accrual on ACT/365; month-end semi-annual coupons of half the annual rate; the books-closed period before a coupon is not modelled | Rand position, cash, accrued interest and settlement; US dollar figures at the current rate for balances and unrealized P&L, at the booking rate for income, commission and realized P&L; FX effects reported separately. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `em_hard_debt` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount; "If cash is short": Fund the shortfall from Treasury, when the Account cannot pay | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none | T+2 from the instrument's own convention on the US dollar payment calendar (OTC, no venue country); Labor Day is skipped. Daily accrual on 30/360; semi-annual coupon to the face settled before the coupon date; next coupon and maturity scheduled | Clean cost at average; accrued interest bought and sold through Accrued income; Treasury funding of the shortfall is an internal transfer (no P&L in the Book, no borrowing), returned by a transfer at the end. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `agency_debt` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount; Order type Limit with a Limit price; Time in force | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none | T+1 on the US bond calendar; each partial fill settles on its own; Martin Luther King Jr. Day is skipped. Working and partly filled orders across matching cycles; a Day order expires at the end of the business day; daily accrual on 30/360; semi-annual coupon | Average cost across partial fills; the commission minimum is carried by the order, not by each fill; accrued interest bought fill by fill; each fill is reconciled with the confirmed figures in the history. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `supranational_bond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none | T+2 from the instrument's own convention on the TARGET calendar (Luxembourg listing; flagged approximate); the 1 May TARGET holiday is skipped. Daily accrual on ACT/ACT with one coupon period a year; annual coupon to the face settled before the coupon date; accrual continues on a TARGET holiday | Euro position, cash, accrued interest and settlement; US dollar figures at the current rate for balances and unrealized P&L, at the booking rate for income, commission and realized P&L; FX effects reported separately. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `municipal_bond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 5,000); Order type Stop with a Stop price, for the protective sale | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none (tax treatment is not modelled: no tax-equivalent yield, no tax lot) | T+1 on the US bond calendar; Thanksgiving is skipped; a stop order that triggers later settles T+1 from the day it fills. Daily accrual on 30/360; semi-annual coupon to the face settled before the coupon date (a sale settling on the coupon date leaves the coupon with the seller); a resting stop order triggers on the last price | Clean cost at average, premium included and not amortised; realized P&L on each sale; coupon income separate; per-bond commission. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `sovereign_sukuk` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 200,000) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none (the Terminal calls the periodic distribution a coupon and the dissolution a redemption) | T+2 from the instrument's own convention on the US dollar payment calendar; Columbus Day is skipped; a sale that would settle after the dissolution date is refused. Daily accrual of the profit rate on 30/360; periodic distribution on the coupon schedule, paid on the next business day when the date is a weekend; final distribution and face amount paid together at dissolution | Clean cost at average; the premium paid over par is a realized loss at dissolution, not amortised; distributions are coupon income, apart from realized P&L. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `corporate_bond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 1,000); Sell from: the lot to reduce, when the Account holds two | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | Tender offer: redemption price, face amount and label, recorded by hand on the position (Lifecycle, Early redemption) | T+1 on the US bond calendar; Good Friday is skipped; an early redemption is paid on the day it is recorded. Daily accrual on 30/360 (US) from the dated date; a short first coupon to the face settled before the coupon date; a lot bought for settlement on the coupon date starts its schedule with the next coupon; early redemption by hand | Each lot at its own clean average cost; realized P&L on a sale and on the tender; interest income separate, including the accrued interest paid with the tender. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `corporate_bond_ig` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 1,000) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none: coupon schedule, 30/360 accrued interest and settlement follow from the registered terms | T+1 on the US bond calendar (Book setting settlement.bond = 1); a coupon date on Martin Luther King Jr. Day is paid the next business day. Daily accrual on 30/360 (US); semi-annual coupon to the face settled before the coupon date, paid on the next payment day after a holiday; next coupon and maturity scheduled | Clean cost at average across two purchases; cost removed at the average on the sale; accrued interest bought and sold through Accrued income; coupon income separate from realized and unrealized P&L; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `high_yield_bond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 1,000) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | Default: coupon suspension recorded on the instrument (Edit, Lifecycle events recorded by hand, Review coupon suspension, Suspend coupons); Restructuring: early redemption at the cash distribution price, face and label, recorded on the position (Lifecycle, Early redemption) | T+1 on the US bond calendar; a sale of the defaulted bond settles flat (no accrued interest); the restructuring distribution is paid on the day it is recorded. Daily accrual on 30/360 until the coupon suspension; on suspension, accrued interest reversed (non-accrual); a coupon date passing while suspended is listed as not paid; restructuring by hand closes the position | Clean cost at average; accrued interest reversed out of coupon and interest income on suspension; realized losses on the flat sale and on the restructuring distribution; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `eurobond` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount (a multiple of 100,000) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | none: annual coupon, 30E/360 accrued interest and T+2 TARGET settlement follow from the registered terms and conventions | T+2 on the TARGET calendar (instrument convention, settlement lag 2); Good Friday and Easter Monday are skipped; amounts in euro. Daily accrual on 30E/360 (a 31st counts as the 30th); one coupon a year on 15 March; next coupon and maturity scheduled | Euro position, cash, accrued interest and settlement; US dollar figures at the current rate for balances and unrealized P&L, at the booking rate for income, commission and realized P&L; FX effects reported separately. Collateral: None for a long position |

| `syndicated_loan` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action (Buy or Sell: no short sale of a loan); Face amount (original principal); Settlement (a stated date when the assignment settles late) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; TSOFR3M fixing of each interest period (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); delayed compensation from the standard (T+7) and actual settlement dates, the coupon rate and the reference rate | Registration: Trading documentation par, Settlement convention 7, Coupon Floating TSOFR3M + spread, Current coupon, Pool factor; The settlement date of a late assignment, on the ticket; Assignment fee (manual cash flow, Fee); Amortisation and voluntary prepayment recorded by hand as paydowns from the agent's notices | T+7 on the US bond calendar (par documentation); a later settlement date pays delayed compensation to the buyer, settled with the trade. Automatic: daily accrual on the principal outstanding, quarterly interest, reset from the fixing, delayed compensation. Manual: amortisation, prepayment, assignment fee. Refused: a short sale (a loan cannot be borrowed). Not modelled: borrower interest-period elections other than quarterly, commitment fees on undrawn amounts, the agent's transfer records | Cost per 100 of principal outstanding; delayed compensation and interest in coupon and interest income; amortisation and prepayment at par against cost realized; assignment fee in fees; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position (the loan's own security package is the lenders', not modelled) |
| `leveraged_loan` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action (Buy or Sell: no short sale of a loan); Face amount (original principal) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; TSOFR3M fixing of each interest period (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData) | Registration: Trading documentation par, Settlement convention 7, Coupon Floating TSOFR3M + 3.50%, Current coupon, Pool factor; Quarterly amortisation recorded by hand as a paydown; Repricing recorded by hand on the instrument form (New spread, Repricing premium, Review repricing, Record repricing) | T+7 on the US bond calendar (par documentation), Juneteenth skipped. Automatic: daily accrual on the principal outstanding, quarterly interest, reset from the fixing plus the spread in force. Manual: amortisation, the repricing (new spread from the interest period that starts on the payment date, soft-call premium). Refused: a repricing recorded off an interest payment date. Not modelled: a mid-period repricing with split interest, non-consenting lenders replaced | Cost per 100 of principal outstanding; interest and the repricing premium in coupon and interest income; amortisation at par against cost realized; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `private_credit` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action (Buy or Sell: no short sale of a loan); Face amount; State a fill price (no market quote) | TSOFR3M fixing of each interest period (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); accrued interest at the cash rate plus the PIK rate; the PIK part of each coupon capitalised; settlement date (same day); commission from the Book fee schedule | Registration: Coupon Floating TSOFR3M + 6.00%, PIK rate 0.02, Undrawn commitment, Current coupon, Settlement convention 0; Fill prices stated on the ticket (no quote); Quarterly valuations entered by hand (Instrument drawer, Enter a price by hand); Draw recorded by hand from the position (Lifecycle, Draw funded); Commitment fee recorded by hand (manual cash flow, Coupon); Refinancing recorded by hand as an early redemption at 101 | Same day (settlement convention 0). Automatic: daily accrual at the cash plus PIK rate, quarterly interest split into cash and PIK (principal and cost up), reset from the fixing. Manual: valuations (no market price), the draw (on an interest payment date), the commitment fee, the refinancing. Refused: a draw off an interest payment date (a stub period is not modelled) | Cost per 100 of principal outstanding, PIK capitalised into cost; interest income in cash and in kind, commitment fee as interest income; OID and the 101 repayment through realized P&L; commission expensed; value from the manual mark. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `distressed_debt` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action; Face amount | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest while performing; none once in default (flat); settlement date T+20 from the instrument convention and the US bond calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule | Registration: Trading documentation distressed, Settlement convention 20; Default recorded by hand (instrument form: Review coupon suspension, Suspend coupons); Recovery distributions recorded by hand (Recovery distribution per 100, Distribution interim or final) | T+20 on the US bond calendar (distressed documentation), MLK Day and Memorial Day skipped; flat once in default. Automatic: accrual and coupons while performing; coupon dates in default listed as not paid. Manual: the default (non-accrual), the interim and final recovery distributions. Refused: trading after the final distribution. Not modelled: distributions in new securities (record them as a sale at the distribution value and a purchase of the new instrument) | Purchased accrued interest reversed out of coupon income on default; interim distribution lowers cost (cost recovery); final distribution and the sale realized; commission expensed. Collateral: None for a long position |
| `loan_participation` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Account, Action, Face amount, order terms, Settlement), then the trade preview. Required: Account; Action (Buy or Sell: no short sale); Face amount (the participation's share of the grantor's face) | bid, ask, last in % of par (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; accrued interest to the settlement date from the registered coupon terms; settlement date from the instrument convention and settlement calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; TSOFR3M fixing of each interest period (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); the participation's share of each cash flow, passed through after the payment delay | Registration: Grantor, Face held by the grantor, Payment delay 2, Trading documentation par, Settlement convention 10, Coupon Floating TSOFR3M + 3.25%, Current coupon; Amortisation recorded by hand as a paydown passed through on the payment date | T+10 on the US bond calendar, Presidents' Day skipped. Automatic: daily accrual, quarterly interest passed through two days after the coupon date with the share of the grantor's amount in the history, reset from the fixing. Manual: amortisation (passed through as a receivable until the payment date). Not modelled: the grantor's own default (exposure noted on the instrument), voting rights | Cost per 100 of principal outstanding; interest income; amortisation at par against cost realized; commission expensed. Collateral: None (an unsecured claim on the grantor for the pass-through) |
