## Forward / FRA (`forward`)

8 products, 8 specified in `test/matrix/specs/forward.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| TBA mortgage contract (`tba_mortgage`) | UMBS 30-year 5.5% TBA for June 2026: 5,000,000 original face bought at 100.25, variation margin posted, pool allocated by hand (a 6% pool refused), delivered (simulated) on 11 June, pool sold | 11 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 15.6 s |
| Equity forward (`equity_forward`) | Equity forward: 10,000 Ridgeway Industrial shares held and sold forward at 52.40, physically delivered (simulated) on 17 April 2026, fixed independent amount | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 0.9 s | complete, 12.8 s |
| Bond forward (`bond_forward`) | Bond forward: 2,000,000 face of the UST 4% Nov-2030 bought forward at 99.25 for value 18 May 2026, delivered (simulated) with accrued interest, then sold | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 12.9 s |
| FX forward (`fx_forward`) | EUR/USD deliverable forward, bought and sold for value 22 June 2026, position-level collateral, held across Juneteenth, unfunded then funded on the value date | 16 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.3 s | complete, 18.8 s |
| Non-deliverable forward (`ndf`) | USD/INR non-deliverable forward sold by Treasury, cash-settled in USD against the 30 April 2026 fixing, partly terminated early, uncollateralized | 10 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 13.4 s |
| Commodity forward (`commodity_forward`) | Copper forward: 250 tonnes bought at 9,450 USD a tonne, cash-settled against the 15 July 2026 close, variation margin posted and returned | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 13.8 s |
| Forward-rate agreement (`fra`) | EUR 3x6 FRA on 3M EURIBOR, bought at 2.15% under a Book collateral agreement, partly terminated early, settled at the start of the period against the fixing. The contract names a collateral agreement of the Book, so a step registers it (Instruments, New instrument) once the agreement exists; it is then traded from the instrument drawer. | 14 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 15.8 s |
| FX swap (`fx_swap`) | EUR/USD FX swap: buy 1,000,000 EUR for 3 June and sell them back for 6 July 2026, near and far legs in one package, uncollateralized | 7 | complete, 0.1 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 10.8 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tba_mortgage` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Forward on an asset); Settlement (Physical delivery); Pool coupon; Value date (SIFMA settlement date); Counterparty; Collateral basis and its terms; Account; Action (Buy); Original face; State a fill price (% of par); pool allocation: Allocated pool | TBA mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication); pool bid and ask (quote fixture); accrued interest and current face of the delivered pool from its registered terms; variation margin from the position-level terms | dealt TBA price (stated fill price); registration of the allocated pool (Instruments, New instrument: agency MBS with its factor and payment delay); pool allocation recorded on the position (Lifecycle, Pool allocation) | No cash at entry; variation margin posted and returned; on the settlement date the allocated pool is delivered (simulated) against the price on current face plus accrued interest; the pool sale settles T+3. Variation margin (automatic); pool allocation by hand, with good-delivery checks (a pool of another coupon is refused); delivery of the allocated pool on the settlement date (automatic once allocated, waiting visibly otherwise) | Nil cost; TBA value = original face x (mark - dealt) / 100; the delivered pool carried at the dealt price on current face, accrued interest bought as an asset; the pool sale realizes against it. Collateral: Position-level terms: no independent amount; variation margin, threshold 0, minimum transfer 25,000 USD; returned when the contract settles |
| `equity_forward` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Forward on an asset); Underlying (the stock); Settlement (Physical delivery); Multiplier (shares per unit); Value date; Counterparty; Collateral basis and its terms; Account; Action (Sell); quantity (shares); State a fill price | stock bid and ask (quote fixture); forward mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication); commission from the Book fee schedule; independent amount from the position-level terms | dealt forward price (stated fill price) | No cash at entry other than the independent amount; on the value date the shares held are delivered at the dealt price (simulated, labelled), delivery against payment that day; forward commission paid on the value date. Physical delivery on the value date (automatic), from the shares the Account holds; without them the delivery waits visibly (engine test) | Nil cost; forward value = quantity x (mark - dealt); the delivery is a sale of the shares at the dealt price, realizing against their cost. Collateral: Position-level terms: fixed independent amount 20,000 USD posted at entry and returned at delivery; no variation margin |
| `bond_forward` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Forward on an asset); Underlying (the bond); Settlement (Physical delivery); Value date; Counterparty; Collateral basis and its terms; Account; Action (Buy); Face amount; State a fill price (% of par, clean) | forward mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication, % of par); note bid and ask (quote fixture); accrued interest at delivery from the note's coupon schedule (ACT/ACT); independent amount from the position-level terms; commission from the Book fee schedule | dealt clean forward price (stated fill price) | No cash at entry other than the independent amount; on the value date the note is delivered against payment of the clean price plus accrued interest (simulated, labelled); the later sale of the note settles T+1. Physical delivery on the value date (automatic); the delivered note then accrues and is sold | Nil cost; forward value = face x (mark - dealt) / 100; the delivered note carried at the dealt clean price with the accrued interest paid as an asset. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 1% of notional posted at entry and returned at delivery; no variation margin |
| `fx_forward` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Deliverable FX forward); Base currency; Quote currency; Value date; Counterparty; Collateral basis and its terms; Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Base-currency amount; State a fill price | value-date check on the joint calendar of both currencies; independent amount and variation margin from the position-level terms; commission from the Book fee schedule; mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication); EUR/USD rate for the reporting currency (FX fixture) | dealt forward rate (stated fill price); early termination amount (position Lifecycle menu, "Early termination") | No cash at entry other than the independent amount; commission and the exchange of 1,000,000 EUR against 1,085,000 USD on the value date, only when the USD is in settled cash; otherwise a visible failure retried each cycle. Daily variation margin on the mark (automatic); early termination of the short at a stated amount (manual); settlement by exchange of both currencies on the value date (automatic), failed while unfunded and completed once funded | Nil cost; value and unrealized P&L = quantity x (mark - dealt rate) in USD; the termination amount is realized; after delivery the EUR is a cash balance valued at the EUR/USD fixture in the reporting currency. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 2% of notional posted at entry and returned at termination or settlement; variation margin, threshold 0, minimum transfer 2,000 USD; collateral received is restricted cash with a liability |
| `ndf` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Non-deliverable forward); Base currency; Quote currency; Value date; Fixing date; Settlement currency; Fixing source (currency pair); Counterparty; Collateral basis (Uncollateralized); Trading owner (Treasury); Action (Sell); quantity; State a fill price | mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication); USD/INR rate for the reporting currency (FX fixture); the fixing: close of the USD/INR pair on the fixing date (fixture standing in for the reference rate) | dealt forward rate (stated fill price); early termination of 2,000,000 at a stated amount in USD (position Lifecycle menu) | No cash at entry; cash settlement in USD on the value date of quantity x (fixing - dealt) / fixing, booked when the fixing arrives; the termination amount settles two business days after it is agreed. Settlement against the fixing (automatic), waiting visibly while the fixing is missing and completed when it arrives; early termination of part of the notional at a stated amount (manual) | Nil cost; value and unrealized P&L in INR = quantity x (mark - dealt), converted to USD at the fixture; termination amount and settlement amount realized in USD. Collateral: Explicitly uncollateralized: no independent amount, no variation margin |
| `commodity_forward` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Forward on an asset); Underlying (the copper instrument); Settlement (Cash against a fixing); Value date; Counterparty; Collateral basis and its terms; Account; Action (Buy); Tonnes; State a fill price (USD a tonne) | forward mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication); the fixing: copper close on the value date (close fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); independent amount and variation margin from the position-level terms; commission from the Book fee schedule | dealt forward price (stated fill price) | No cash at entry other than the independent amount; on the value date 250 x (fixing - dealt) in cash, waiting visibly while the fixing is missing; commission paid on the value date. Daily variation margin on the mark (automatic); cash settlement against the fixing (automatic) | Nil cost; value = tonnes x (mark - dealt); the settlement amount is realized. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 5% of notional; variation margin, threshold 10,000 USD, minimum transfer 5,000 USD, posted by the Account and returned; everything returned when the contract settles |
| `fra` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: kind of forward, its terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Account, Buy / Sell, quantity, State a fill price); the strategy instance for early termination. Required: Kind of forward (Forward-rate agreement); Interest period start; Interest period end; Fixing date; Reference rate code; Day count; Counterparty; Collateral basis (Agreement); Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Notional; State a fill price (contract rate) | mark (quote fixture standing in for a dealer indication, % a year); EURIBOR3M fixing (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); EUR/USD rate (FX fixture) for the collateral exposure and the reporting currency; variation margin from the agreement | contract rate (stated fill price); collateral agreement (Treasury, Collateral, New agreement); early termination of 5,000,000 at a stated amount | No cash at entry; at the start of the period N x (L - K) x d / (1 + L x d) in EUR against the fixing of 1 June, waiting visibly while the fixing is missing; the termination amount settles on the date agreed. Variation margin under the agreement (automatic); early termination of part of the notional (manual); settlement at the period start against the fixing (automatic) | Nil cost; value = N x (mark - K) / 100 x 92/360 in EUR, converted to USD at the fixture; termination and settlement amounts realized in EUR. Collateral: Book agreement CSA Dealer C: no independent amount; variation margin in USD, threshold 0, minimum transfer 5,000 USD; received collateral is restricted cash with a liability; returned when the contract ends |
| `fx_swap` | Registry form for each leg (Kind of forward: Deliverable FX forward, its own value date, required Collateral terms); then the Strategies page, template Custom, Add a leg (registered instrument) twice, Preview package: both legs confirmed together. Required: each leg: Kind of forward, Base currency, Quote currency, Value date, Counterparty, Collateral basis; Trading owner; Execution template (Custom); each leg: Instrument, Side, Amount in EUR, State a fill price | marks of each leg (quote fixtures standing in for dealer indications); EUR/USD rate for the reporting currency (FX fixture) | near and far rates (stated fill prices) | No cash at entry; the near leg exchanges 1,000,000 EUR against 1,085,000 USD on 3 June, the far leg 1,000,000 EUR against 1,087,200 USD on 6 July, each only when the paying currency is held. Two value dates, each settled automatically; nothing remains after the far leg | Nil cost; after the near leg the euros are a cash balance valued at the EUR/USD fixture and the far leg a forward worth -1,000,000 x (mark - 1.0872): together they do not move with the exchange rate; the forward points are the result. Collateral: Both legs explicitly uncollateralized |

## Cash loan / deposit (`loan`)

9 products, 9 specified in `test/matrix/specs/loan.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Certificate of deposit (`certificate_of_deposit`) | Meridian Bank certificates of deposit held by an Account: 500,000 USD at 5.10% to 1 June 2026, and 100,000 USD at 4.80% redeemed early. A certificate of deposit is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket (here opened from the Account page, side "Lend or deposit cash") and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. | 13 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 0.9 s | complete, 23.7 s |
| Bank deposit (`bank_deposit`) | Northgate Bank call deposit of an Account: 200,000 USD at 3.60%, open-ended, interest paid monthly. A bank deposit is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket (opened from the Account page, side "Lend or deposit cash") and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. | 14 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 26.8 s |
| Term deposit (`term_deposit`) | Sterling term deposit placed by Treasury with Kingsway Bank: 400,000 GBP at 4.20% fixed, ACT/365, to Good Friday 3 April 2026 (paid 7 April). A term deposit is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket of the Treasury page (side "Lend or deposit cash") and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. | 13 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 21.8 s |
| Revolving credit facility (`revolving_credit_facility`) | Northbridge Capital 1,000,000 USD revolving facility of an Account: two drawings at 6.30%, a part repayment and a redraw, the commitment fee recorded by hand. A facility drawing is not a registry instrument: each drawing is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket (from the Account page, "Borrow cash", product Revolving credit facility) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. | 11 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 21.0 s |
| Cash borrowing and lending (USD or foreign currency) (`cash_loan`) | Treasury borrows 360,000 EUR at 3.00% and converts it to dollars at spot (T+2 over Good Friday and Easter Monday); an Account lends 150,000 USD for two weeks. A cash loan is not a registry instrument: the borrowing is entered on the Borrow and convert ticket of the Treasury page and the lending on the Cash loan or deposit ticket of the Account page; each registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The EUR/USD pair it converts through is a registered spot FX instrument. | 20 | complete, 0.4 s | complete, 1.3 s | complete, 34.8 s |
| Secured loan (`secured_loan`) | Calder Trust Bank loan to an Account, secured on 4,000 of its 5,000 Harrow Ridge shares: 120,000 USD at 5.40%, interest monthly, repaid late for want of cash. A secured loan is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket (from the Account page, "Borrow cash"), with the position it pledges, and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The shares are a registered common stock. | 16 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 30.5 s |
| Unsecured loan (`unsecured_loan`) | Harborline Bank unsecured term loan to an Account: 250,000 USD at 6.00% fixed, ACT/360, due 1 April 2026. An unsecured loan is not a registry instrument: the contract is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket (here from the Account page, "Borrow cash") and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. | 19 | complete, 0.4 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 31.9 s |
| Margin loan (`margin_loan`) | Kestrel Aerospace shares bought on margin by an Account: 50,000 USD borrowed at 7.20% from the security ticket, open-ended; repaid in part and in full. A margin loan is not a registry instrument: it is a leg the security ticket adds when "Borrow the shortfall" is chosen, registered when the package is confirmed. The shares are a registered common stock. | 16 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 28.4 s |
| Securities-backed loan (`securities_backed_loan`) | Ashcombe Private Bank loan to Treasury against 8,000 Brightwater shares: 300,000 USD at SOFR + 1.50%, open-ended; a missing fixing blocks the interest payment until it is entered by hand. A securities-backed loan is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Cash loan or deposit ticket of the Treasury page, with the position it pledges, and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The shares are a registered common stock. | 16 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 28.1 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `certificate_of_deposit` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly) | interest start date; daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run, ACT/360 (no run on a market holiday; the next run covers the days); principal and interest returned at maturity | every term of the certificate (issuer, principal, rate, day count, maturity), entered on the ticket | None: the cash leaves settled cash on the day the ticket is confirmed and returns at maturity or on redemption. Interest accrues daily; principal and interest are returned at maturity. Early redemption in full is allowed with interest to the day and no penalty. A secondary-market sale is not supported and is refused | Booked as cash lent to the issuing bank: an asset of the Account, carried at principal; interest is income accrued daily. Cash placed in a CD is not available to trade, transfer or withdraw. Collateral: None |
| `bank_deposit` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly) | interest start date; daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run; interest paid on the first business day of each month, and the next payment date | every term of the deposit, entered on the ticket (no maturity: open-ended); a change of rate by the bank, entered by hand with Set rate; the amount of a part withdrawal, entered in the preview | None: cash leaves and returns to settled cash at once. Interest accrues daily and is paid monthly on the first business day; part and full withdrawal at any time, with interest to the day settled on a full withdrawal. A deposit is not enlarged: a further deposit is a new record (resizing is refused) | Cash lent is an asset of the Account; interest is income. A transfer between two Accounts is internal to the Book: no P&L, eliminated in the Book balance sheet. Collateral: None |
| `term_deposit` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly) | interest start date; daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run, ACT/365; the payment date: the stated maturity moved to the next business day of the sterling payment calendar; principal and interest returned at maturity; conversion of each entry to the reporting currency at the rate in force (GBP/USD from a labelled fixture) | every term of the deposit, entered on the ticket; the amount of an early part withdrawal, entered in the preview | None: the cash leaves settled sterling on the day the ticket is confirmed and returns on the payment date. Interest accrues daily; at maturity principal and interest are returned automatically. A maturity on a bank holiday is paid on the next business day with interest to that day. An early withdrawal, in part or in full, is allowed with interest to its date and no penalty (a break cost would be recorded by hand) | Cash lent is an asset of Treasury; interest is income, accrued daily in sterling and converted at the rate of each day; the change in the dollar value of the sterling balances is the FX effect. Collateral: None |
| `revolving_credit_facility` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly) | interest start date of each drawing; daily interest accrual on each drawing at the end-of-day run; repayment of each drawing with its interest at its maturity | every term of each drawing, entered on the ticket; the amount of a part repayment, entered in the preview; the commitment fee on the undrawn amount, worked out by the desk and recorded by hand as a fee on a drawing (strategy instance, position Lifecycle: Manual cash flow); the facility limit: tracked by the desk, not by the Terminal | None: each drawing is settled cash at once; repayments leave settled cash at once. Each drawing is its own loan: interest accrues daily and is paid with the principal at its maturity; part repayment at any time; a redraw is a new drawing (a new record). The facility limit and the commitment fee are not simulated (catalog: partial) | Each drawing is a liability of the Account; interest is a funding expense; the commitment fee is a fee, shown on the drawing it was recorded on (fees to date). Collateral: None (unsecured) |
| `cash_loan` | Treasury page, "Borrow and convert" (the loan leg and its spot conversion, one preview and one confirmation); "Convert currency" for the conversion back; an Account page, "Borrow cash" with side "Lend or deposit cash" for cash lent; Repay on the row of the borrowing. Required: Borrower (Treasury or an Account); Currency pair and the currency borrowed; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid; Counterparty | interest start date (the confirmation date); the spot value date: two TARGET2 business days, also a US dollar banking day; the conversion price: bid or ask of the quote in force (fixture); daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run; settlement of both currencies on the value date; conversion of each entry to US dollars at the rate in force (fixture); principal and interest returned at maturity | every term of the loan, entered on the ticket; the amount of euros bought back, entered on the Convert currency ticket | The loan has none: borrowed cash is settled cash at once. The conversion settles on its spot value date (T+2 on the joint calendar): until then the currency bought is buying power but cannot be withdrawn or transferred, and the currency sold is committed. Interest accrues daily; early repayment in full with interest to the day; a repayment needs settled cash of the loan currency and is refused without it; cash lent is returned with its interest at maturity | Borrowed euros are a liability of Treasury (carried at principal, converted at the current rate); interest is a funding expense; the cost of crossing the spread is part of the FX effect; cash lent is an asset of the Account and its interest is income. Collateral: None (unsecured) |
| `secured_loan` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly); Collateral position (a long position of the borrower); Quantity to pledge | interest start date; daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run; interest paid on the first business day of each month; repayment of principal and interest at maturity, retried each cycle while cash is short; release of the pledged shares on repayment in full | every term of the loan, entered on the ticket; the position pledged and the quantity, entered on the ticket | None for the loan: borrowed cash is settled at once. The shares bought and sold around it settle T+1. Interest accrues daily and is paid monthly; principal is repaid at maturity with the interest since the last payment. A repayment the borrower cannot fund fails visibly, keeps accruing and is retried; it is made once cash arrives | Borrowed cash is a liability of the Account; interest is a funding expense; the pledged shares stay on the Account balance sheet at market value. Collateral: Named shares pledged: the pledged quantity cannot be sold (or lent, or pledged again) until the loan is repaid in full, when it is released |
| `unsecured_loan` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly) | interest start date (the day the ticket is confirmed); daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run; repayment of principal and interest at maturity; the interest settled with a repayment in full | every term of the contract (principal, rate, day count, maturity, lender), entered on the ticket; a rate change, entered by hand with Set rate; the amount of a part repayment, entered in the preview | None: borrowed cash is settled cash on the day the ticket is confirmed; a repayment leaves settled cash at once. Interest accrues daily; principal and accrued interest are repaid at maturity (1 April 2026) unless repaid earlier, which is allowed in part or in full with interest to the repayment date | Cash borrowed is a liability of the Account that borrowed it; interest is a funding expense accrued daily; the borrowing is one record, on the Account balance sheet, in Treasury oversight as Account-originated, and once in the Book. Collateral: None (unsecured) |
| `margin_loan` | Instrument drawer, Trade: the security ticket with "If cash is short: Borrow the shortfall (simulated lender)" and its loan rate; the margin loan is a leg of the same preview and confirmation. Repay and Set rate on the row of the borrowing (Account page). Required: Account; Action (buy); Quantity; If cash is short: borrow the shortfall; Loan rate (fixed, decimal a year) | the amount borrowed: the package's cash shortfall; the lender (simulated); interest start date; daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run; interest paid on the first business day of each month (open-ended) | the loan rate, typed on the ticket; a rate change by the lender, entered with Set rate; the amount of a part repayment, entered in the preview | The loan has none (settled cash at once, ahead of the purchase it funds); the shares settle T+1. A repayment needs settled cash: sale proceeds count only once settled. Open-ended: interest accrues daily and is paid monthly; repaid in part or in full at any time with interest to the day; the rate can be reset by hand | Borrowed cash is a liability of the Account; interest is a funding expense; the shares bought are an asset at market value. Collateral: Secured on the Account's holdings as a whole: no named pledge, so the shares stay sellable (no margin call is simulated) |
| `securities_backed_loan` | Treasury page, "New loan or deposit" (or an Account page, "Borrow cash"): the Cash loan or deposit ticket, then the trade preview; Repay or Withdraw and Set rate on the row of the open arrangement. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (borrow, or lend / deposit); Product; Currency; Principal; Rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Maturity (empty for open-ended); Interest paid (at maturity or monthly); Collateral position (a long position of the borrower); Quantity to pledge | interest start date; the day's reference-rate fixing (fixture; a fixing covers at most a week); daily interest accrual at the end-of-day run; interest paid on the first business day of each month; the stop of accrual, the flag on the row and the block on the payment when no fixing is available; release of the pledged shares on repayment in full | every term of the loan, entered on the ticket (reference rate code and spread); the position pledged and the quantity; a missing fixing, entered by hand (Data connection, Manual entries) | None for the loan; the shares bought and sold settle T+1 on the US calendar (the sale of Thursday 2 April settles Monday 6 April, after Good Friday). Interest accrues daily at the day's fixing plus the spread and is paid monthly; without a fixing interest stops, the payment waits (blocked, with its reason) and repayment is refused until the fixing is entered or the rate is set by hand; repayment in full at any time with interest to the day | Borrowed cash is a liability of Treasury; interest is a funding expense; the pledged shares stay on Treasury's balance sheet at market value. Collateral: Named shares pledged: not sellable until the loan is repaid in full, then released |

## Currency balance (`cash`)

1 product, 1 specified in `test/matrix/specs/cash.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| USD and foreign currency balances (`currency`) | Euro balances in a USD Book: deposit, Treasury funding, conversion both ways, transfers that never convert, FX effect, withdrawal. A currency balance is ledger cash, not a registry instrument (the registry refuses product "currency", asserted in the first step). The scenario holds euro balances in Treasury and the Account and converts them through a registered EUR/USD spot pair. | 15 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 16.8 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `currency` | Treasury page: "Deposit or withdraw" (capital), "Transfer cash" (between Treasury and an Account); Account page: "Convert currency" (a spot FX trade in a registered pair, through the trade preview). Required: Currency and amount (deposit, withdrawal, transfer); Source and destination (transfer); Currency pair, direction and base amount (conversion) | conversion price: bid or ask of the pair quote (fixture); value date of a conversion (T+2 on both currencies' payment calendars); reporting-currency amount of every entry at the rate in force (FX fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData) | every amount, entered on the dialogs | Deposits, withdrawals and transfers move settled cash at once; a conversion settles both currencies on its value date, with pending pay and receive legs until then. None: balances have no lifecycle; the reporting-currency value follows the FX rate | Capital is not P&L; a transfer creates no Book P&L and never converts; FX effect = translation of euro balances plus the dealing cost of each conversion; unsettled currency is not withdrawable or transferable. Collateral: None |

The 43,192 USD bought settles on 4 March; until then it is buying power, not cash that can leave the Account. |

## Securities loan (`secloan`)

4 products, 4 specified in `test/matrix/specs/secloan.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Securities borrowing (`securities_borrowing`) | Securities borrowing of 10,000 ETF units by an Account for a short sale: 105% cash collateral marked daily, fee on market value, part return, lender recall and forced buy-in. A securities borrowing is not a registry instrument: it is the borrow leg of the Sell short action on a registered security and registers itself when the sale is confirmed. Here the security is a registered ETF (fund units, so the borrow is a securities borrowing rather than a stock loan). | 14 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 27.4 s |
| Securities lending (`securities_lending`) | Securities lending by Treasury of 15,000 ETF units to Pellham Securities: 102% cash collateral restricted and owed back, fee income, distribution as a manufactured payment, recalled in two parts. Securities lending is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Lend a security ticket of the Treasury page from a position held, and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The units lent are a registered ETF. | 15 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 29.8 s |
| Stock loan (`stock_loan`) | Stock loan by an Account of 8,000 Xetra-listed shares to Vossberg Bank: 105% euro collateral, fee received monthly, held over Easter, manufactured dividend, reporting-currency figures, recalled. A stock loan is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Lend a security ticket of the Treasury page from a position held (here the Account's), and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The shares are a registered Xetra-listed common stock. | 11 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 24.4 s |
| Bond loan (`bond_loan`) | Bond loan by Treasury of 2,000,000 Ashgrove Paper 4.5% notes: cash collateral at 102% of dirty value marked daily, fee on clean value, coupon kept by the lender, recalled. A bond loan is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Lend a security ticket of the Treasury page from a bond position held, and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The bonds are a registered corporate bond. | 10 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 21.7 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `securities_borrowing` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab: Sell short (adds the securities borrow leg; availability and fee from the borrow fixture), then Buy to cover (adds the return leg); the strategy instance's position Lifecycle menu for a lender recall. Required: Account; Action (Sell short / Buy to cover); Units; borrow assumption only when no borrow data is supplied | borrow availability and fee (borrow fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); the cash collateral at 105% of market value and the 25% margin reserve (Book short settings); the daily fee accrual on market value; the daily re-mark of the collateral; the forced buy-in and return when a recall deadline passes; the fee paid when the borrow is fully returned | the lender's recall (quantity and business days to return), recorded with Lender recall on the borrow position | T+1 on the US equity calendar: the sale's proceeds settle into restricted cash; a cover is paid from restricted cash first; collateral left over is released. Daily fee accrual and collateral mark; part return with a part cover; a recall sets a deadline (alert); at the deadline the uncovered short is bought in and the securities returned; the fee is paid on full return (otherwise monthly) | The borrow is one record in the borrowing register (securities, their value, the fee); the short position carries the proceeds as negative cost; the fee is a borrowing expense; realized P&L on each cover. Collateral: Restricted cash = 105% of market value, marked daily; 25% margin reserve; neither is buying power; released as the short is covered |
| `securities_lending` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "Lend a security": the Lend a security ticket (lender, product, position to lend, quantity, fee, cash collateral share, recallable, counterparty), then the trade preview; Take back on the row of the loan in the lending table. Required: Lender (Treasury or an Account); Product; Position to lend (a long position held); Quantity to lend; Fee (decimal a year); Cash collateral (share of value); Recallable; Counterparty | the cash collateral at 102% of market value (price fixture); the daily lending fee accrual on market value; the daily re-mark of the collateral; the monthly fee received on the first business day; the collateral returned pro rata on a recall, and the fee to date received on the last | every term, entered on the ticket; the distribution (ex-date, amount), recorded by hand; each recall (Take back), with its quantity | None for the loan: the collateral is received and returned in restricted cash when the loan is opened, marked and taken back; the units bought settle T+1. Fee accrues daily and is received monthly and on the full return; collateral marked daily; distributions on the units lent are received as manufactured payments; recall in part or in full by hand | The units lent stay Treasury's holding at market value (on loan, not sellable); the cash collateral is restricted cash with a matching liability (Cash collateral received); the fee is lending income; the distribution is dividend income. Collateral: Cash 102% of market value, held as restricted cash and owed back; marked daily; returned pro rata on a partial recall and in full on the last |
| `stock_loan` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "Lend a security": the Lend a security ticket (lender, product, position to lend, quantity, fee, cash collateral share, recallable, counterparty), then the trade preview; Take back on the row of the loan in the lending table. Required: Lender (Treasury or an Account); Product; Position to lend (a long position held); Quantity to lend; Fee (decimal a year); Cash collateral (share of value); Recallable; Counterparty | the cash collateral at 105% of market value, in euros (price fixture); the daily fee accrual on market value, calendar days over holidays; the daily re-mark of the collateral; the fee received on the first business day of the month and on the full return; conversion of every entry to dollars at the rate in force (EUR/USD fixture) | every term, entered on the ticket (product Stock loan, collateral share 1.05); the dividend (ex-date, amount), recorded by hand; the recall (Take back) | None for the loan: collateral received and returned in restricted euros; the shares bought settle T+2 on the Xetra calendar. Fee accrues daily (over Easter too) and is received monthly and on return; the dividend on shares lent is received as a manufactured payment; recall by hand | The shares lent stay the Account's holding (on loan, not sellable); the euro collateral is restricted cash with a matching liability; fee income and dividend income are booked in dollars at each day's rate; the change in the dollar value of the euro balances is the FX effect. Collateral: Cash 105% of market value (cross-border stock loan), restricted and owed back; returned in full on the recall |
| `bond_loan` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "Lend a security": the Lend a security ticket (lender, product, position to lend, quantity, fee, cash collateral share, recallable, counterparty), then the trade preview; Take back on the row of the loan in the lending table. Required: Lender (Treasury or an Account); Product; Position to lend (a long position held); Quantity to lend; Fee (decimal a year); Cash collateral (share of value); Recallable; Counterparty | the cash collateral at 102% of the dirty value (price fixture plus accrued interest from the registered coupon terms); the daily re-mark of the collateral as interest accrues and after the coupon; the daily fee accrual on the clean market value; the coupon on the bonds lent, paid to the lender; the collateral returned and the fee to date received on the recall | every term, entered on the ticket (product Bond loan); the recall (Take back) | None for the loan: collateral received, marked and returned in restricted cash; the bonds bought settle T+1. Fee accrues daily on clean value; collateral marked daily on dirty value; the coupon is paid to the lender (manufactured on the face lent); fee received monthly and on return; recall by hand | The bonds lent stay Treasury's holding at market value with their accrued coupon (on loan, not sellable); the collateral is restricted cash with a matching liability; the fee is lending income; the coupon is coupon income. Collateral: Cash 102% of dirty value (a bond loan's convention), restricted and owed back; it rises as interest accrues, falls when the coupon is paid, and is returned on the recall |

## Repo / reverse repo (`repo`)

11 products, 11 specified in `test/matrix/specs/repo.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Repo (`repo`) | Two-week repo of 2,000,000 Ostrander Rail 5% notes by an Account with Kittering Securities: cash = dirty value less a 2% haircut, 4.40% ACT/360, repurchased on the end date. A repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (for the Account) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered corporate bond. | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 0.9 s | complete, 22.9 s |
| Reverse repo (`reverse_repo`) | Two-week reverse repo by Treasury against 5,000,000 US Treasury 3.75% notes: cash capped at dirty value less 2%, collateral held not owned, margin call and return of excess by hand, terminated on the end date. A reverse repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered US Treasury note. | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 25.8 s |
| Overnight repo (`overnight_repo`) | Overnight repo of 10,000,000 US Treasury bills by Treasury, repurchased the next business day and rolled by hand over Memorial Day. An overnight repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered US Treasury bill. | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 0.9 s | complete, 20.5 s |
| Term repo (`term_repo`) | Sterling term repo of 2,000,000 UK gilts by an Account in a dollar Book: haircut and buffer, ACT/365, coupon in the term kept by the borrower, margin call met, repurchase after Easter with reporting-currency figures. A term repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (for the Account) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered UK government bond (gilt). | 12 | complete, 0.4 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 26.9 s |
| Open repo (`open_repo`) | Open repo of 50,000 shares by an Account: no end date, rate reset by hand, termination on demand refused for want of cash, then terminated with interest at each rate. An open repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (for the Account) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered NYSE-listed common stock. | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 27.1 s |
| Bilateral repo (`bilateral_repo`) | Bilateral repo by an Account with Pemberton Bank: collateral substituted by hand, the automatic repurchase failing for want of cash and made the next day once funded. A bilateral repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (for the Account) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered corporate bond, substituted by a registered agency bond. | 11 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 26.7 s |
| Tri-party repo (`triparty_repo`) | One-week tri-party repo by Treasury with a money-market fund through Northbridge Collateral Services: part of a holding pledged, excess returned by hand after a rise, repurchased on the end date. The collateral is a registered corporate bond. | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 0.9 s | complete, 21.1 s |
| General-collateral repo (`gc_repo`) | General-collateral repo of 2,500,000 US Treasury notes by an Account at SOFR + 0.05%: fixings stop, the repurchase waits for a fixing entered by hand. A GC repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (for the Account) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered US Treasury note. | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 21.6 s |
| Special-collateral repo (`special_repo`) | Special repo of 5,000,000 on-the-run ten-year US Treasury notes by Treasury at a special rate of 1.25%: substitution refused, repurchased on the end date. A special repo is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (collateral type Special) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The collateral is a registered US Treasury note. | 7 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 0.8 s | complete, 16.9 s |
| Buy/sell-back (`buy_sell_back`) | Buy/sell-back by Treasury of 2,000,000 Marlow Energy 6% notes: forward price fixed at inception, coupon in the term received by the buyer and taken off the forward, rate reset and collateral change refused. A buy/sell-back is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (Reverse repo side, product Buy/sell-back) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The security is a registered corporate bond. | 7 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 0.8 s | complete, 16.6 s |
| Sell/buy-back (`sell_buy_back`) | Sell/buy-back by an Account of 3,000,000 Corbridge Mills 5.5% notes: forward price fixed at inception, the coupon in the term passed to the buyer, part repurchase refused, bought back at the forward amount. A sell/buy-back is not a registry instrument: it is entered on the Repo or reverse repo ticket of the Treasury page (Repo side, product Sell/buy-back, for the Account) and registers itself when the ticket is confirmed. The security is a registered corporate bond. | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 21.1 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the collateral's dirty value on the start date (price fixture plus accrued interest from the registered coupon terms); the most cash it supports after the haircut (a larger principal is refused); repo interest accrued daily at the end-of-day run; repurchase on the end date: principal and interest paid, the collateral released; the daily check of the cover | every term of the repo, entered on the ticket (counterparty, haircut, rate, end date); the cash principal, entered on the ticket (at most the collateral value less the haircut) | None for the repo: cash is received on the day it is confirmed and paid back on the end date. The bond bought to pledge settles T+1. Interest accrues daily; on the end date the repo is repurchased automatically with interest and the collateral released; the pledged notes keep accruing their coupon for the Account | Cash borrowed is a liability of the Account; repo interest is a funding expense; the pledged notes stay on the Account's balance sheet at market value with their accrued coupon. Collateral: The notes are pledged for the whole term: not sellable or lendable; released on repurchase. Cover checked daily (margin calls not simulated) |
| `reverse_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the collateral's dirty value on the start date (price fixture plus accrued interest from the registered coupon terms); the most cash it supports after the haircut (more is refused); repo interest income accrued daily at the end-of-day run; the daily check of the cover (flagged when short, cleared when restored); termination on the end date: principal and interest received, the collateral returned | every term of the reverse repo, entered on the ticket; the cash lent; a margin call met (collateral delivered) and a return of excess collateral, recorded with Collateral on the reverse repo's row | None: the cash leaves settled cash on the day the ticket is confirmed and comes back on the end date. Interest accrues daily; the cover is checked daily; collateral delivered or returned is recorded by hand; terminated automatically on the end date | Cash lent is an asset of Treasury; repo interest is income ("Coupon and interest income"); the collateral received is held, not owned: never a position, a holding or a balance-sheet asset, listed as collateral received. Collateral: Securities received, valued dirty; cover = value less 2% haircut against principal plus interest; a shortfall is flagged daily; a margin call and a return of excess are recorded by hand, the return refused when it would leave the cover short |
| `overnight_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the end date: the next business day of the dollar payment calendar (Tuesday after Memorial Day); the bills' value from the price fixture (a discount bill accrues nothing); the most cash it supports after the haircut; repurchase on the end date with interest for the days run; the daily check of the cover | every term, entered on the ticket; the roll: a new overnight repo entered each day at that day's rate (the Terminal does not renew an overnight repo by itself) | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed, paid back on the next business day. The bills bought settle T+1. Repurchased automatically on the next business day with interest; rolled by entering a new overnight repo; an end date over a holiday runs to the next business day with interest for every day | Cash borrowed is a liability of Treasury; repo interest is a funding expense; the pledged bills stay on Treasury's balance sheet at market value. Collateral: The bills are pledged for each night: not sellable or lendable; released at each repurchase and pledged again by the roll |
| `term_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open); Additional buffer (amount), when the terms state one | the gilts' dirty value (price fixture plus ACT/ACT accrued interest from the registered coupon terms); the most cash it supports: value less the haircut less the buffer; repo interest accrued daily on ACT/365; the coupon of the pledged gilts paid to the Account (the borrower keeps it in a classic repo); the daily check of the cover; the repurchase date: the stated end date moved to the next sterling business day; conversion of every entry to dollars at the rate in force (GBP/USD fixture) | every term, entered on the ticket (counterparty, haircut, buffer, rate, end date); the cash principal; the margin call met: more gilts pledged with Collateral on the repo's row | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed and paid back on the repurchase date. The gilts bought settle T+1. Interest accrues daily; the coupon is paid to the borrower during the term; the cover is checked daily; repurchased automatically on the first sterling business day on or after the end date, with interest to that day | Cash borrowed is a liability of the Account, in sterling, shown in dollars at the current rate; repo interest is a funding expense booked in dollars at each day's rate; the pledged gilts stay on the Account's balance sheet with their accrued coupon; the change in the dollar value of the sterling balances is the FX effect. Collateral: The gilts are pledged: not sellable or lendable; cover = dirty value less 3% less 25,000 GBP against principal plus interest; the coupon lowers the dirty value and the shortfall is flagged; more gilts pledged by hand; all released on repurchase |
| `open_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the shares' value from the price fixture; the most cash it supports after the haircut; repo interest accrued daily at the rate in force; the daily check of the cover; the cash a termination needs: principal and interest to the day | every term, entered on the ticket (Term: Open, no end date); the new rate, entered with Set rate on the repo's row; the termination on demand, with Repurchase on the repo's row | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed and paid back when terminated. The shares bought settle T+1. Runs until terminated: no end date and nothing scheduled; interest accrues daily at the rate in force; a rate reset accrues to its date at the old rate first; termination on demand pays principal and all interest | Cash borrowed is a liability of the Account; repo interest is a funding expense; the pledged shares stay on the Account's balance sheet at market value. Collateral: The shares are pledged until the repo is terminated: not sellable or lendable; cover checked daily |
| `bilateral_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the collateral's dirty value and the cash it supports; repo interest accrued daily; the substitute's cover checked against what is owed; the repurchase on the end date, and its retry each cycle after it failed for want of cash, with interest to the day it is made | every term, entered on the ticket (Tri-party left unticked); the substitution agreed with the counterparty, recorded with Collateral on the repo's row; internal funding of the Account from Treasury | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed and paid back at the repurchase. The bonds bought settle T+1. Interest accrues daily; repurchased automatically on the end date; when the borrower lacks the cash the repurchase fails visibly (failed lifecycle item, alert), interest keeps accruing, and it is retried every cycle until made | Cash borrowed is a liability of the Account; repo interest a funding expense; the pledged notes stay on the Account's balance sheet with their accrued coupon. Collateral: Pledged notes not sellable; substituted by hand (the old notes released, the new pledged, the cover rechecked); released on repurchase. Bilateral and tri-party are simulated alike: the agent of a tri-party repo is recorded as a term only |
| `triparty_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open); Tri-party (ticked) and the tri-party agent | the notes' dirty value and the cash they support after the haircut; repo interest accrued daily; the cover after the return of excess checked against what is owed; repurchase on the end date with interest; the daily check of the cover | every term, entered on the ticket, Tri-party ticked and the agent named; the excess collateral returned by the agent, recorded with Collateral on the repo's row (the agent's allocation and substitutions are not simulated) | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed and paid back on the end date. The notes bought settle T+1. Interest accrues daily; excess collateral returned by hand; repurchased automatically on the end date | As a bilateral repo: cash borrowed is a liability of Treasury; repo interest a funding expense; the pledged notes stay on Treasury's balance sheet. Collateral: Part of the holding pledged (2,500,000 of 3,000,000 face), the rest free; a return of excess leaves 2,400,000 pledged; released on repurchase. Tri-party differs from bilateral only by the agent recorded as a term: basket allocation and automatic substitution by the agent are not simulated |
| `gc_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the notes' dirty value and the cash supported; each day's SOFR fixing (fixture; a fixing covers at most a week); repo interest accrued daily at the fixing plus the spread; the stop of accrual, the flag and the alert when no fixing is available; the repurchase on the end date, waiting (blocked, with its reason) until the fixing is supplied or entered | every term, entered on the ticket (floating: reference rate SOFR and spread; collateral type General collateral); a missing SOFR fixing, entered by hand (Data connection, Manual entries) | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed and paid back at the repurchase. The notes bought settle T+1. Interest accrues daily at SOFR + 0.05%; without a fixing it stops and the repurchase waits; a repurchase by hand is refused meanwhile; once the fixing is entered the waiting repurchase is made at once | Cash borrowed is a liability of the Account; repo interest a funding expense; the pledged notes stay on the Account's balance sheet with their accrued coupon. Collateral: General collateral (any eligible Treasury), recorded as a term: the notes pledged, released on repurchase. Nothing else differs from special collateral in the Terminal |
| `special_repo` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (overnight, term with its end date, open) | the notes' dirty value and the cash they support after the haircut; repo interest accrued daily at the special rate; repurchase on the end date; the daily check of the cover | every term, entered on the ticket: collateral type Special and the special rate (the Terminal does not derive a special rate) | None for the repo: cash received when confirmed and paid back on the end date. The notes bought settle T+1. Interest accrues daily at the special rate; repurchased automatically on the end date | Cash borrowed is a liability of Treasury; repo interest at the special rate is a funding expense; the notes stay on Treasury's balance sheet. Collateral: The named security, pledged: not sellable; a substitution is refused (a special repo is done for that security; a GC repo's collateral can be substituted by hand); released on repurchase |
| `buy_sell_back` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (Term, with the forward date); Structure (Buy/sell-back); Repo rate (fixed only) | the notes' dirty value and the cash they support after the haircut; the forward amount and forward price (clean and dirty), fixed at inception from the rate and the coupon falling in the term; repo interest accrued daily; the coupon received during the term (reduces the cash lent); the resale on the forward date at the forward amount | every term, entered on the ticket (product Buy/sell-back on the Reverse repo side, fixed rate, forward date) | None simulated as securities delivery: the spot leg pays the cash when confirmed; the forward leg returns the forward amount on the forward date. Interest accrues daily; the coupon paid during the term is received by the buyer and reduces what is lent; on the forward date the forward amount fixed at inception is received; a rate reset and any collateral change are refused | Accounted as cash lent against the notes (an asset; the difference between forward and spot, with the coupon, is repo interest income); the notes are held, not owned, and are not on Treasury's balance sheet. Collateral: The notes bought are held for the term (listed as collateral received); no margin, return or substitution: the trade is two outright trades at prices fixed at inception |
| `sell_buy_back` | Treasury page, Borrowings and lending, "New repo or reverse repo": the Repo or reverse repo ticket (borrower or lender, side, product, collateral position or security, currency, cash principal, terms), then the trade preview; Set rate and Repurchase (or Terminate) on the row of the open repo. Required: Borrower or Lender (Treasury or an Account); Side (repo or reverse repo); Product; Collateral position (repo) or Collateral security (reverse repo); Collateral quantity; Haircut; Currency; Cash principal; Repo rate (fixed: the rate; floating: reference rate code and spread); Day count; Term (Term, with the forward date); Structure (Buy/sell-back); Repo rate (fixed only) | the notes' dirty value and the cash they support; the forward amount and price (clean and dirty) fixed at inception, net of the coupon falling in the term; repo interest accrued daily; the coupon paid by the issuer to the Account and passed to the buyer the same day (reduces what is owed); the repurchase on the forward date at the forward amount | every term, entered on the ticket (product Sell/buy-back on the Repo side, fixed rate, forward date) | None simulated as securities delivery: the spot leg brings the cash when confirmed; the forward leg pays the forward amount on the forward date. The notes bought beforehand settle T+1. Interest accrues daily; the coupon paid during the term is passed to the buyer; repurchased automatically on the forward date at the forward amount; a part repurchase, a rate reset and any collateral change are refused | Accounted as a financing: cash received is a liability of the Account; the notes stay on its balance sheet with their accrued coupon (coupon income is the Account's); the coupon passed on reduces the liability; forward less spot, with the coupon, is repo interest expense. Collateral: The notes sold are pledged for the term: not sellable; no margin, return or substitution; released on the forward date |
