## Spot asset (`spot`)

14 products, 14 specified in `test/matrix/specs/spot-commodities.mjs`, `test/matrix/specs/spot.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Physical / spot commodity (`physical_commodity`) | Uranium concentrate (U3O8) on a converter account: held in kilograms, priced per pound (x 2.2046226 lb/kg), storage and insurance by hand | 12 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 15.6 s |
| Precious metal (`precious_metal`) | Allocated gold kilobars in a London vault, held by Treasury: kilograms priced per troy ounce (x 32.1507466), settlement over the May bank holiday | 14 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 17.2 s |
| Industrial metal (`industrial_metal`) | Copper cathode, LME Grade A, in a Rotterdam warehouse: tonnes in 25-tonne lots, warehouse rent by hand | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 15.6 s |
| Crude oil (`crude_oil`) | WTI crude oil at Cushing (pipeline barrels): tank storage, quality bank credit and pipeline tariff by hand | 13 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 17.3 s |
| Refined petroleum product (`refined_product`) | Ultra-low sulphur diesel, New York Harbor barge: barrels priced per US gallon (x 42), barge freight and inspection by hand | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 15.7 s |
| Natural gas (`natural_gas`) | Natural gas at Henry Hub, held by Treasury in storage: MMBtu, next-day settlement, injection and withdrawal fees by hand | 12 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 0.9 s | complete, 15.9 s |
| LNG (`lng`) | LNG cargo share delivered ex-ship in Japan: tonnes priced per MMBtu (x 51.8 from the cargo's quality certificate), boil-off and port charges by hand | 14 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 17.2 s |
| Coal (`coal`) | Steam coal CIF ARA in a Rotterdam stockpile: tonnes, calorific-value adjustment and stockpile storage by hand | 13 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 16.9 s |
| Electricity (`electricity`) | German baseload power for Friday 20 March 2026 on the EPEX SPOT day-ahead auction, in EUR: intraday sale, delivery period recorded by hand | 11 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 15.7 s |
| Agricultural commodity (`agricultural_commodity`) | Hard red winter wheat at a Kansas City elevator: bushels priced in US cents per bushel (x 0.01), storage and protein premium by hand | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 16.1 s |
| Livestock (`livestock`) | Live steers at the Oklahoma National Stockyards: pounds of live weight priced per hundredweight (x 0.01), yardage and transit shrink by hand | 12 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 16.4 s |
| Emissions allowance (`emissions_allowance`) | EU Allowances (EUA, Phase 4) on the EEX spot market in EUR: settlement across Easter, compliance surrender by hand | 14 | complete, 0.4 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 18.5 s |
| Carbon credit (`carbon_credit`) | Verra VCUs, Rio Verde REDD+ vintage 2023, bought by Treasury over the counter: registry delivery T+3, retirement by hand | 10 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 14.2 s |
| Renewable energy certificate (`renewable_energy_certificate`) | Texas wind RECs, vintage 2025 (1 MWh each): T+2 over Memorial Day, retirement by hand | 11 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 14.1 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `physical_commodity` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Quantity in the product's own unit | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | converter storage fee, recorded by hand as a manual cash flow (fee); insurance of material at the converter, recorded by hand (fee) | T+2 (Book setting settlement.spot) on the US calendar, by book transfer at the converter (simulated: the Terminal moves no material). Nothing automated; storage and insurance recorded by hand; delivery out of the converter account is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = kilograms x USD per pound x 2.2046226; average cost per pound; broker commission 10 bp expensed; storage and insurance in fees; realized P&L on sale. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `precious_metal` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner: Treasury); Action; Kilograms (whole bars: lot size 1) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | allocated vault storage, recorded by hand (fee); insurance, recorded by hand (fee) | T+2 (Book setting settlement.foreignCash) on the London calendar: over the Early May bank holiday. Nothing automated; storage and insurance recorded by hand; taking the bars out of the vault is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = kilograms x USD per troy ounce x 32.1507466; average cost per troy ounce; no commission (dealer spread only); realized P&L on sale; owned by Treasury. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `industrial_metal` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Tonnes (lots of 25) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | LME warehouse rent, recorded by hand (fee) | T+2 (Book setting settlement.foreignCash) on the TARGET2 calendar of the Rotterdam venue. Nothing automated; warehouse rent recorded by hand; load-out (physical delivery) is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = tonnes x USD per tonne; commission 1.00 per tonne expensed; rent in fees; realized P&L on sale. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `crude_oil` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Quantity in the product's own unit | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | tank storage, recorded by hand (fee); quality-bank adjustment for API gravity, recorded by hand (realized); pipeline tariff (delivery cost), recorded by hand (fee) | T+2 (Book setting settlement.spot) on the US calendar. Nothing automated; storage, quality and delivery costs recorded by hand; lifting the barrels (physical delivery) is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = barrels x USD per barrel; broker fee 0.02 per barrel expensed; storage and tariff in fees; quality credit in realized P&L. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `refined_product` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Quantity in the product's own unit | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | barge freight (delivery cost), recorded by hand (fee); independent quantity and quality inspection, recorded by hand (fee) | T+2 (Book setting settlement.spot) on the US calendar. Nothing automated; freight and inspection recorded by hand; discharging the barge (physical delivery) is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = barrels x 42 x USD per gallon; freight and inspection in fees; realized P&L on sale. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `natural_gas` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner: Treasury); Action; MMBtu | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | storage injection fee, recorded by hand (fee); storage withdrawal fee, recorded by hand (fee) | T+1 (the instrument's settlement lag) on the US calendar. Nothing automated; storage fees recorded by hand; nominating the gas out of storage (physical delivery) is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = MMBtu x USD per MMBtu; broker fee 0.005 per MMBtu expensed; storage fees in fees; realized P&L on sale; owned by Treasury. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `lng` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Quantity in the product's own unit | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | heat content (51.8 MMBtu per tonne) from the cargo quality certificate, entered as the multiplier; port and regasification terminal charges (cargo logistics), recorded by hand (fee); boil-off lost on the voyage, recorded by hand as a sale at a stated fill price of 0.00 | T+2 (Book setting settlement.foreignCash) on the Tokyo calendar, over Marine Day. Nothing automated; cargo logistics and boil-off recorded by hand; discharge to the terminal (physical delivery) is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = tonnes x 51.8 MMBtu per tonne x USD per MMBtu; port charges in fees; boil-off realizes its cost as a loss; realized P&L on sale. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `coal` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Quantity in the product's own unit | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | calorific-value (quality) adjustment, recorded by hand (realized); stockpile storage, recorded by hand (fee) | T+2 (Book setting settlement.foreignCash) on the TARGET2 calendar of the Rotterdam venue. Nothing automated; quality adjustment and storage recorded by hand; loading the coal out (physical delivery) is not simulated (refused, visibly) | Value = tonnes x USD per tonne; broker fee 0.10 per tonne expensed; storage in fees; quality adjustment in realized P&L. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `electricity` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; quantity in the product's own unit), then the trade preview; position Lifecycle menu for manual cash flows. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; MWh | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; value and cash = quantity x price x multiplier (unit conversion); settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule | delivery of the energy to the offtaker after the delivery period, recorded by hand as a sale at the agreed price (State a fill price); imbalance charge for the delivery day, recorded by hand (fee) | T+1 (the instrument's settlement lag) on the TARGET2 calendar of the venue. Collateral: None; no short sale |

| `renewable_energy_certificate` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short; optional Settlement stated for the trade), then the trade preview. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Quantity in the asset's own unit (whole units: lot size 1) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date on the venue country's calendar; commission from the Book spot fee schedule (basis points) | retirement against electricity consumed, recorded by hand as a sale at a stated fill price of 0.00 | T+2 (Book setting settlement.spot) on the US calendar: a Thursday purchase settles the next Tuesday over Memorial Day. No automated event; retirement recorded by hand | Average cost per certificate (MWh); broker commission 50 bp expensed; a retirement realizes the cost retired as a loss with no cash; realized P&L on sale. Collateral: None; no short sale |

## Digital asset (`crypto`)

3 products, 3 specified in `test/matrix/specs/crypto.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Cryptocurrency (`cryptocurrency`) | Halcyon (HLC), a native cryptocurrency on a crypto trading platform: weekend fills, fractional units, bps fees | 15 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 19.8 s |
| Stablecoin (`stablecoin`) | Eurolink euro token (EURL), a EUR stablecoin on a Belgian crypto platform, in a USD-reporting Book | 9 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 12.8 s |
| Tokenized asset (`tokenized_asset`) | Meridian On-chain Treasury Token (MOTT): a tokenized short-term Treasury fund, income recorded by hand | 10 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.0 s | complete, 14.2 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cryptocurrency` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short), then the trade preview. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Units (fractional to 1e-8) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date on the 24x7 calendar (same day); commission from the Book crypto fee schedule (basis points) | staking reward (units received), recorded by hand as a purchase at a stated fill price of 0.00; forks and airdrops the same way | Same day (Book setting settlement.crypto = 0) on the 24x7 calendar (ALLDAYS): weekend trades settle on the weekend. None automated; staking rewards, forks and airdrops recorded by hand | Average cost; commission in basis points expensed; realized P&L on sale; valued at the last price; units received for nothing carried at zero cost (their value is unrealized gain, not income). Collateral: None; no securities borrow, so no short sale |
| `stablecoin` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short), then the trade preview. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Units (fractional to 1e-8) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date on the 24x7 calendar (same day); commission from the Book crypto fee schedule (basis points); EUR/USD conversion for every reporting figure (FX fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData) | none (the peg is observed in the quote, not assumed) | Same day on the 24x7 calendar, in EUR from the Account's euro balance; the Sunday sale settles on the Sunday. None automated | Average cost in EUR; commission in EUR converted at the posting rate; realized P&L in EUR converted at the sale date rate; FX effect = translation of euro balances at cost. Collateral: None; no short sale |
| `tokenized_asset` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (security ticket: Buy, Sell; no Sell short), then the trade preview. Required: Account (trading owner); Action; Units (fractional to 1e-8) | bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; settlement date on the 24x7 calendar (same day); commission from the Book crypto fee schedule (basis points) | income on the underlying (the fund's monthly distribution), recorded by hand as a manual cash flow (dividend) on the position; network (gas) fee of an on-chain transfer, recorded by hand as a manual cash flow (fee) | Same day on the 24x7 calendar (Book setting settlement.crypto = 0). No event automated; income of the underlying and network fees recorded by hand | Average cost; commission in basis points expensed; manual income in dividends, network fee in fees; realized P&L on sale; valued at the last price. Collateral: None; no short sale |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `equity_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts; registration: Underlying (the deliverable stock), Settlement Physical delivery | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | final settlement price, entered by hand on the contract (Overview, "Enter a price by hand", closing price for the last trading day) | No purchase cash; initial margin 1,500 USD a contract; daily variation margin; at expiry the contracts close at the final settlement price and 100 shares a contract are delivered (simulated, paper entry) at that price; the invoice settles T+1 like any stock purchase. Daily variation margin (automatic); final settlement waits, visibly blocked, until a final settlement price exists, then closes the contracts and simulates the delivery of the shares (automatic, labelled simulated) | Nil cost; variation margin and closes are realized P&L; commission 0.75 USD a contract with a 5.00 USD minimum an order; without a price the position is unpriced and the net asset value provisional; delivered shares carried at the final settlement price. Collateral: Initial margin per contract, posted in cash, released on reduction, close and final settlement |
| `equity_index_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | none: the final settlement price is supplied as the close of the last trading day (it can be entered by hand, see equity_future) | No purchase cash. Initial margin 22,000 USD a contract is posted on the trade date; variation margin is paid or received in cash at each end-of-day pass; cash final settlement on the last trading day. Daily variation margin (automatic); final settlement in cash at the final settlement price on the last trading day (automatic); roll to the next contract month from the strategy instance; a contract past its last trading day refuses trades | Nil cost; value is open trade equity since the last settlement; variation margin and closes are realized P&L; commission 2.25 USD a contract expensed; margin posted is the Account's own asset. Collateral: Initial margin per contract from the contract terms, held in "Margin and collateral posted": not available to trade or to withdraw; released pro rata on reduction, close and final settlement. An order whose margin is not covered is refused |
| `gov_bond_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | none in this scenario (delivery of a deliverable bond is not simulated: a delivery notice is refused with that reason, and the position is closed before the last trading day) | No purchase cash; initial margin 2,600 EUR a contract and daily variation margin, both in euros; no variation on a TARGET holiday. Daily variation margin on the contract's own business days (automatic); none on 1 May 2026 (TARGET holiday, US business day); the first settlement after the holiday is against the last settlement before it | Euro cash, margin and open trade equity translated to USD at the current fixture rate; realized P&L and commission at the rate of their day; the rest is FX effects; commission 1.50 EUR a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract in EUR, posted from the Account's EUR cash; released pro rata |
| `treasury_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Daily variation margin (automatic); a delivery notice is refused because the deliverable basket, conversion factors and accrued interest are not modelled; on the last trading day closed in cash at the final settlement price, labelled "physical delivery is not simulated" (automatic); a contract past that day refuses trades | Nil cost; prices in sixty-fourths carried exactly; variation margin and closes are realized P&L; commission 0.85 USD a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract, released pro rata on reduction and at final settlement |
| `stir_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account (here: Treasury); Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | none: the final settlement price (100 minus the compounded rate) is supplied as the close of the last trading day | No purchase cash; initial margin 550 USD a contract from Treasury's cash; daily variation margin at 25 USD a basis point; cash final settlement at 100 minus the rate. Daily variation margin (automatic); cash final settlement on the last trading day (automatic); a contract past that day refuses trades | Treasury's own position, P&L and margin; the Account is untouched; the Book total includes it once; commission 1.20 USD a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract posted from Treasury's settled cash: not transferable to an Account while posted |
| `fx_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Daily variation margin (automatic); on the last trading day closed in cash at the final settlement price, labelled "physical delivery is not simulated" (automatic); a contract past that day refuses trades | Yen cash, margin and open trade equity translated to USD at the current USD/JPY fixture; realized P&L and commission at the rate of their day; the rest is FX effects; commission 240 JPY a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract in JPY; released pro rata on reduction and at final settlement |
| `commodity_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts; registration: Underlying (the deliverable), Settlement Physical delivery, Deliverable units per contract, First notice day, Deliverable grade, Delivery location; delivery notice: Contracts to deliver | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | delivery notice (position Lifecycle menu, "Delivery notice"): refused here for the long side, before the window, for more than held and on a closed position; storage, transport and inspection costs of delivered oil are not simulated (would be manual cash flows) | No purchase cash; initial margin 6,000 USD a contract; daily variation margin at 10 USD a cent; no pass on Good Friday; held to the last trading day: simulated physical delivery at the final settlement price, invoice owed T+2 on the deliverable's own settlement terms; the delivered barrels sold and settled T+2 across Memorial Day. Daily variation margin (automatic); no end-of-day pass on a US market holiday; roll from the strategy instance (close May, open June, one confirmation); partial close; at the last trading day simulated delivery (automatic, labelled simulated); delivery notices refused with a specific reason when outside the contract's terms | Nil cost; variation margin, closes and the final settlement are realized P&L; delivered barrels carried at the invoice amount and then sold (realized); commission 1.50 USD a contract and 0.01 USD a barrel. Collateral: Initial margin per contract; the roll releases the May margin and posts the June margin in one package; the partial close and the final settlement release margin |
| `volatility_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | funding of a cash deficit after variation margin: a Treasury transfer made by the user (the Terminal never funds it by itself) | No purchase cash; initial margin 8,000 USD a contract; daily variation margin at 1,000 USD a point; cash final settlement on the Wednesday. Daily variation margin (automatic), paid even when it exceeds free cash: settled cash goes negative and a cash-deficit alert is raised; cash final settlement at the special opening quotation (automatic) | Nil cost; variation margin and closes are realized P&L; a deficit is shown as negative settled cash, never as a silent loan or transfer; commission 1.80 USD a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract; a variation call beyond free cash is a visible deficit; new orders are refused while it stands; the funding transfer creates no P&L |
| `dividend_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | none: the final value of the dividend index is supplied as the close of the last trading day | No purchase cash; initial margin 1,100 USD a contract; daily variation margin at 250 USD a dividend point; cash final settlement at the realized dividend index. Daily variation margin (automatic); cash final settlement on the third Friday of December (automatic); a contract past that day refuses trades | Nil cost; no dividend income is booked (the dividends are the price); variation margin and closes are realized P&L; commission 1.10 USD a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract; released pro rata on reduction and at final settlement |
| `crypto_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | none: the final reference rate is supplied as the close of the last trading day | No purchase cash and no bitcoin: initial margin 95,000 USD a contract; daily variation margin at 5 USD a dollar of price; cash final settlement at the reference rate. Daily variation margin on exchange business days (automatic); cash final settlement on the last Friday of the month (automatic); a contract past that day refuses trades | Nil cost; no digital asset is held; variation margin and closes are realized P&L; commission 6.00 USD a contract. Collateral: Initial margin per contract; an order whose margin is not covered by free cash is refused |
| `perpetual_future` | Instrument drawer, Trade tab (futures ticket: Account, Buy or Sell, Contracts), then the trade preview; the strategy instance for Resize, Close and Roll. Required: Account; Action (Buy / Sell); Contracts | bid, ask and last (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); fill price and fill model; initial margin from the contract terms; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; daily settlement price (close fixture) | each funding payment, recorded by hand: strategy instance, the position's Lifecycle menu, Manual cash flow, kind "Funding expense" (negative when paid, positive when received) | No purchase cash; initial margin 700 USD a contract; variation margin at each end-of-day pass (US business days); no expiry and no final settlement. Variation margin (automatic, not on weekends); funding payments are not generated and are recorded by hand; no expiry item exists; closes like any future | Nil cost; variation margin and closes are realized P&L; funding paid and received under borrowing and funding expenses; commission 5 basis points of contract value. Collateral: Initial margin per contract; released pro rata on reduction and close |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `listed_option` | Strategies page (execution template Protective Put) for the opening package; the contract's Trade tab for the part sale (Close half on the position); then the trade preview. Required: Account; Execution template; Instrument; Shares; Expiration; Put strike | premium bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); underlying price for the notional and for the reserve of an uncovered call (quote fixture); fill price and fill model; premium settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; cash reserved against written options from the Book margin assumption; underlying close for the expiration date (close fixture); EUR/USD rate (FX fixture) for reporting-currency figures; contracts from the share quantity and the contract's deliverable (500 shares / 100) | stock split of the underlying (ratio, ex-date), recorded by hand on the share | Premium T+1 and shares T+2 on TARGET: the shares bought on Wednesday 1 April settle on Tuesday 7 April, after Good Friday and Easter Monday; cash settles in euros. A whole-number forward split of the underlying adjusts the contract on its ex-date (contracts x2, strike / 2, contract size unchanged); expiry item on the expiration date; long put in the money: exercised automatically, shares held in the same strategy instance delivered at the strike (simulated delivery) | Average premium in euros; every entry also stored in US dollars at the rate when posted; balances translated at the current rate, the difference reported as FX effect; premium realized on sale and on exercise. Collateral: None: a long option and the shares it protects reserve nothing |
| `equity_option` | Underlying drawer, Options and futures tab, registered contract (or the contract in its Marketplace); its Trade tab (option ticket), then the trade preview. Close, Resize, Roll and the Lifecycle menu are on the strategy instance. Required: Account; Action (Buy, Sell / write); Contracts | premium bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); underlying price for the notional and for the reserve of an uncovered call (quote fixture); fill price and fill model; premium settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; cash reserved against written options from the Book margin assumption; underlying close for the expiration date (close fixture) | early exercise of a long American option (contracts), recorded by hand from the position Lifecycle menu | Premium T+1 on the US calendar (Book setting settlement.option = 1); shares delivered on exercise settle T+1 at the strike (settlement.equity = 1). Expiry item on the expiration date, blocked until the underlying close exists; in the money: exercised automatically with a simulated delivery of 100 shares a contract at the strike; early exercise by hand; trades refused after expiration | Average premium; commission expensed; premium realized on sale, on exercise and on lapse; shares delivered are carried at the strike; long and written positions of one contract held gross. Collateral: An uncovered written call reserves 20% of the value of the underlying shares in its strategy instance, marked at each end of day and released when it is bought back |
| `etf_option` | Underlying drawer, Options and futures tab, registered contract (or the contract in its Marketplace); its Trade tab (option ticket), then the trade preview. Close, Resize, Roll and the Lifecycle menu are on the strategy instance. The covered call is assembled on the Strategies page (execution template Covered Call). Required: Account; Action (Buy, Sell / write); Contracts; Strategies page: Account, execution template, instrument, shares, expiration, call strike; Roll dialog: new expiration, new strike | premium bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); underlying price for the notional and for the reserve of an uncovered call (quote fixture); fill price and fill model; premium settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; cash reserved against written options from the Book margin assumption; underlying close for the expiration date (close fixture) | early assignment of a written American option (contracts), recorded by hand from the position Lifecycle menu | Premium T+1 on the US calendar: a trade on Thursday 2 April settles Monday 6 April, Good Friday being a market holiday; shares delivered on assignment settle T+1 at the strike. Expiry item on each expiration date; written call in the money: assigned automatically, the shares held against it are delivered at the strike (simulated delivery); written put out of the money: lapses; early assignment by hand; roll to a later expiry in one package | Premium received is a negative cost until the option is bought back, assigned or lapses, when it is realized; shares delivered are carried at the strike; a roll realizes the difference on the contract it closes. Collateral: A written put reserves its full strike value (cash-secured: contracts x 100 x strike), re-sized when contracts are assigned or rolled and released when the put is gone; a call written against shares in the same strategy instance reserves nothing; reserved cash is not buying power |
| `index_option` | Underlying drawer, Options and futures tab, registered contract (or the contract in its Marketplace); its Trade tab (option ticket), then the trade preview. Close, Resize, Roll and the Lifecycle menu are on the strategy instance. The settlement value is entered on the index reference instrument (Overview, Enter a price by hand, with its date). Required: Account; Action (Buy, Sell / write); Contracts | premium bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture); index level for the notional and for the reserve of an uncovered call (quote fixture, last value only); fill price and fill model; premium settlement date; commission per contract; cash reserved against the written call | the settlement value of an AM-settled series (special opening quotation), entered by hand as the index fixing for the expiration date; registration: the index level is carried by a reference instrument, because the catalog has no index product | Premium T+1 on the US calendar; the cash settlement amount at expiry is due on the next business day (Monday 20 April for the Friday 17 April expiry). Expiry items wait, blocked, until the settlement value for the expiration date exists; then settled in cash at 100 x (settlement value - strike) for a call in the money, nothing delivered; out of the money lapses; early exercise and early assignment refused (European) | Average premium; the cash settlement amount closes the position and the difference to the premium is realized; Treasury's puts are on Treasury's own balance sheet and P&L and in the Book's. Collateral: The uncovered written call reserves 20% of 100 x the index level in its strategy instance, marked at each end of day and released at settlement |
| `option_on_future` | The contract in its Marketplace (US Derivatives), Trade tab (option ticket), then the trade preview. Exercise is on the position Lifecycle menu; the futures position delivered is closed with Close on the strategy instance. Required: Account; Action (Buy, Sell / write); Contracts | premium bid, ask, last and size (quote fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); underlying price for the notional and for the reserve of an uncovered call (quote fixture); fill price and fill model; premium settlement date from the listing venue calendar; commission per contract from the Book fee schedule; cash reserved against written options from the Book margin assumption; underlying close for the expiration date (close fixture); initial margin per futures contract from the contract terms; daily settlement price of the future (close fixture) | contract data at registration: premium multiplier (the future's multiplier), deliverable (1 future); early exercise of a long American option (contracts), recorded by hand from the position Lifecycle menu | Premium T+1 on the US calendar; the futures position delivered on exercise takes effect the same day and posts its initial margin at once. Expiry item on the option expiration date against the close of the future; in the money: exercised automatically into a futures position at the strike (simulated delivery) with initial margin posted; out of the money: lapses; the future then settles variation margin daily | Average premium; premium realized on sale, exercise and lapse; the future delivered is carried at the strike as its first settlement price, so the intrinsic value arrives as variation margin. Collateral: A written put reserves contracts x 1,000 barrels x strike (cash-secured); initial margin of 6,500 a futures contract is posted from settled cash on exercise and released when the future is closed |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `interest_rate_cap` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract) | caplet schedule from the contract terms and the USD payment calendar; TSFR3M fixings (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); settlement date of the premium, T+2 on the USD calendar | premium (stated fill price, per 100 notional); mark of the cap, entered by hand; settlement amount of a partial termination (stated fill price in the preview); a fixing the data service has not supplied, entered by hand | Premium and termination amount settle T+2 on the USD calendar; caplet payments are cash on their payment date. Each quarter the caplet pays when the fixing is above the strike and is recorded as nothing due when it is not; a caplet whose fixing is missing is blocked until the fixing is supplied, and maturity waits for it; at maturity the cap ends and the premium still carried is written off | Carried at the premium paid until a mark is entered (provisional), then at the mark; caplet receipts are realized P&L; a partial termination realizes the difference from the average premium; at maturity the remaining premium is a realized loss. Collateral: Uncollateralized (paper assumption): nothing is posted or received, whatever the mark |
| `interest_rate_floor` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract) | floorlet schedule from the contract terms and the USD payment calendar; TSFR3M fixings (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); settlement date of the premium, T+2 on the USD calendar; independent amount from the position-level terms | premium (stated fill price, per 100 notional); mark of the floor, entered by hand; amount paid to buy part of it back (stated fill price in the preview) | Premium and buy-back amount settle T+2 on the USD calendar; floorlet payments are cash on their payment date. Each quarter the seller pays the floorlet when the fixing is below the strike and nothing when it is at or above it (recorded as nothing due); a payment date on a holiday moves to the next business day; at maturity the floor ends and the premium still carried is earned | A sold floor is a negative position carried at the premium received until a mark is entered, then at the mark; floorlet payments are realized P&L; a buy-back realizes the difference from the average premium; at maturity the remaining premium is a realized gain. Collateral: Position-level terms: a fixed independent amount of 100,000 posted when the position opens, unchanged by an increase or a partial buy-back, returned when the position ends; no variation margin |
| `interest_rate_collar` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract) | schedule of both legs from the contract terms and the USD payment calendar; TSFR3M fixings (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); settlement date, T+2 on the USD calendar; variation margin from the position-level terms | net premium (stated fill price, per 100 notional); mark of the collar, entered by hand (negative when the floor is worth more than the cap); settlement amounts of the partial and the full termination (stated fill price in the preview) | Net premium and termination amounts settle T+2 on the USD calendar; leg payments are cash on their payment date. Two legs with their own strikes: the cap leg pays above 4.50%, the floor leg is paid below 3.50%, and between the strikes both legs record that nothing is due; partial termination; full termination before maturity, after which nothing is scheduled | Carried at the net premium until a mark is entered, then at the mark, which can be negative; leg payments and termination results are realized P&L. Collateral: Position-level terms with no independent amount: variation margin at end of day against the mark, threshold 20,000, minimum transfer 5,000; posted when the mark is against the Account, received (restricted, owed back) when it is in its favour, reduced after a partial termination and returned at once on full termination |
| `interest_rate_swap` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract) | payment schedule of each leg from the contract terms and the USD payment calendar; TSFR3M fixings (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); settlement date, T+2 on the USD calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; independent amount and variation margin from the position-level terms | upfront amount of a new position (stated fill price); mark of the contract, entered by hand (it can be negative); a fixing the data service has not supplied, entered by hand; settlement amount of a termination (stated fill price in the preview) | Upfront amount and commission settle T+2 on the USD calendar; leg payments are cash on their payment date. Fixed and floating payments on their adjusted payment dates; a floating payment whose fixing is missing is blocked until the fixing is supplied; increase; partial and full termination at stated amounts; nothing is scheduled after termination | Carried at the mark (per 100 notional) less the upfront amounts paid; leg payments and termination results are realized P&L; commission expensed; no accrual between payment dates (the mark includes it); unpriced and provisional until a mark is entered. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 2% of notional in margin (posted at entry, trued up on increase, returned on termination); variation margin at end of day against the mark, threshold 50,000, minimum transfer 10,000; collateral received is restricted cash with a liability, never spendable |
| `ois` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination; the agreement is recorded first under Treasury, Collateral. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract) | daily SONIA fixings (rate fixture standing in for Shaffer MarketData); compounding over London business days; GBP/USD rate (FX fixture) for reporting-currency figures and for collateral in USD; settlement date, T+2 on the UK calendar; independent amount and variation margin from the agreement | the agreement itself (counterparty, amounts, currencies, haircut); upfront amount (stated fill price); mark of the contract, entered by hand; the one daily fixing the data service did not supply, entered by hand | Upfront amount settles T+2 on the UK calendar; both legs pay once, at maturity. Fixed and compounded overnight amounts at maturity; the compounded payment is blocked while one daily fixing is missing, and maturity waits for it; the position ends at maturity and its collateral is returned | GBP contract in a USD Book: realized P&L at the rate of the payment date, unrealized at the current rate, FX effect on sterling balances; carried at the mark; the upfront amount received is realized at maturity. Collateral: Agreement "CSA Dealer B": independent amount 0.50% of notional and variation margin (no threshold, minimum transfer 25,000 GBP), posted in USD cash at a 2% haircut; the independent amount is trued up when GBP/USD moves; everything is returned at maturity |
| `basis_swap` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract) | payment schedule of each floating leg from the contract terms and the TARGET calendar; EURIBOR3M and EURIBOR6M fixings (rate fixtures standing in for Shaffer MarketData); settlement date, T+2 on TARGET; commission from the Book fee schedule | upfront amount of a new position (stated fill price); mark of the contract, entered by hand; settlement amount of a partial termination (stated fill price in the preview); cash moved from Treasury when a payment cannot be made | Commission and termination amount settle T+2 on TARGET; leg payments are cash on their payment date. Two floating legs, each on its own index, spread and frequency; a payment the Account cannot fund fails visibly and is made once the cash is there; partial termination; final payments and maturity | EUR reporting currency; leg payments and the termination result are realized P&L; commission expensed; carried at the mark once one is entered, provisional before. Collateral: Uncollateralized (paper assumption): nothing is posted or received, whatever the mark |
| `cross_currency_swap` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination. Owner chosen on the ticket: Treasury. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract); each leg: Notional factor, Notional exchanged | payment schedule of each leg; payment dates on the TARGET and Federal Reserve calendars together; principal exchanged at the start, on the settlement of a later change, and back at maturity; EUR/USD rate (FX fixture) for the reporting-currency figures; settlement date, T+2 on TARGET; commission from the Book fee schedule | upfront amount (stated fill price, in euros per 100 of euro notional); mark of the contract, entered by hand, excluding the principal already on the balance sheet; settlement amount of a partial termination; cash moved to Treasury when the principal cannot be paid | Upfront amount and commission settle T+2 on TARGET; principal for a change made after the start moves on that settlement date; leg payments are cash on their payment date. Initial exchange of principal on the effective date (failed visibly for want of dollars, then made once funded); dollar interest semi-annually (moved over Veterans Day); a trade that could not pay its principal is refused; partial termination returns part of the principal on its settlement date; euro interest, last dollar interest, maturity and the final exchange | Principal received is cash and an amount owed; principal paid is an amount lent: neither is P&L. Interest and the termination result are realized P&L at the rate of their day; euro balances are revalued at the current rate and the difference is the FX effect; the figures are Treasury's and the Book's. Collateral: Uncollateralized (paper assumption): nothing is posted or received, whatever the mark |
| `cross_currency_basis_swap` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: legs, schedules, calendars and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase and termination; the agreement is recorded first under Treasury, Collateral. Required: Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, rate or index, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Account; Action; Notional; State a fill price (no executable quote exists for an OTC contract); Trading currency (the currency of the notional and the price); each leg: Notional factor, Spread, Notional exchanged | payment schedule of each leg; payment dates on the Federal Reserve and Tokyo calendars together; TSFR3M and TORF3M fixings (rate fixtures standing in for Shaffer MarketData); principal exchanged at the start, on the settlement of a termination, and back at maturity; USD/JPY rate (FX fixture) for the reporting-currency figures; settlement date, T+2 on the USD calendar; variation margin from the agreement, moved by Treasury | the agreement itself; mark of the contract, entered by hand, in dollars per 100 of dollar notional; settlement amount of a partial termination; cash moved from Treasury when the principal cannot be repaid at maturity | Termination amount settles T+2 on the USD calendar, and the principal of the part terminated with it; leg payments are cash on their payment date; yen in whole yen. Initial exchange on the effective date; yen interest received and dollar interest paid each quarter, each leg on its own index, spread and day count; a payment date that is a holiday in Tokyo moves to the next day and its fixings are the last ones before the holidays; partial termination; at maturity the final exchange fails visibly for want of dollars, and the swap matures once the Account is funded | Principal paid is an amount lent (in yen), principal received an amount owed (in dollars); interest is realized P&L at the rate of its day; yen balances are revalued at the current rate and the difference is the FX effect; price amounts (mark, termination) are in dollars, the trading currency stated on the contract. Collateral: Agreement "CSA Dealer F" covering the Account and Treasury, posted by Treasury: variation margin in full, in USD, from Treasury's cash against the Account's swap; reduced after the partial termination; returned at maturity |

Both legs blocked visibly until the late fixing is entered, then paid in USD; partial termination; the second period on the reduced notional with its fixing entered in advance; maturity with collateral returned. The Terminal has no Mumbai calendar: the USD calendar stands in (stated) | USD contract: leg payments realized in USD at the fixing; carried at the mark; termination result realized; commission expensed; no INR balance at any time. Collateral: Position-level terms: a fixed independent amount of 150,000 USD posted at entry, returned at maturity; no variation margin |

Nothing is paid during the life of the swap; at maturity the fixed amount, compounded annually, is paid; the inflation amount waits, visibly, for the index fixing of the maturity date and is received once that is entered; the swap then matures | Carried at the upfront amounts paid until a mark is entered, then at the mark; the two maturity payments and the termination result are realized P&L; commission expensed. Collateral: Uncollateralized (paper assumption): nothing is posted or received, whatever the mark |

| `constant_maturity_swap` | Strategies page: Execution template "Custom", Add a leg, "New OTC contract", product, contract name, currency, the contract form (dates, counterparty, Collateral terms, each leg), Side, Notional, State a fill price; Preview package. Required: Account; Execution template; Leg: New OTC contract; Product; Contract name; Currency; Effective date; Maturity; each leg: Side, Leg type, Currency, Index, Spread, Payment, Day count; Collateral basis; Side (Enter as written / Enter the opposite side); Notional; State a fill price | market view (US Derivatives) and calendars (US market calendar; payments on it and the Federal Reserve calendar together): the ticket has no fields for them; payment schedule of each leg; TSFR3M and USDCMS5Y fixings (rate fixtures standing in for Shaffer MarketData); settlement date, T+2; independent amount and variation margin from the agreement | the agreement itself; the whole contract, typed into the ticket; upfront amount (stated fill price); mark of the contract, entered by hand; the CMS fixing the data service did not supply, entered by hand; settlement amount of a partial termination | Upfront and termination amounts settle T+2 on the US market calendar; leg payments are cash on their payment date. Two floating legs: one on the money-market index plus a spread, one on the CMS rate, each paid on its own; a payment date on a Federal Reserve holiday moves to the next day; the CMS payment whose fixing is missing is blocked until it is entered; partial termination; maturity | Carried at the mark; leg payments and the termination result are realized P&L; no convexity adjustment is made, and none is implied by the figures. Collateral: Agreement "Clearing terms Broker D" (cleared): independent amount 1% of notional, trued up on increase and partial termination; variation margin in full each day, received when the mark is in the Account's favour (restricted, owed back) and posted when it is against it; everything returned at maturity |

Index return and fee each month on a notional reset monthly; the last reset falls on a Saturday and takes Friday's close, which is missing: the index payment and the maturity wait visibly (the fee is paid) until it is entered by hand; held to maturity; nothing left afterwards | Carried at the mark; index and fee payments are realized P&L; commission expensed. Collateral: Uncollateralized (paper assumption): nothing is posted or received, whatever the mark |

## Credit default swap (`cds`)

7 products, 7 specified in `test/matrix/specs/cds.mjs`.

### Scenarios

| Product | Scenario | Steps | Engine | API | Browser |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Single-name credit-default swap (`cds_single_name`) | Standard five-year single-name CDS, 100 bp, bought, increased, partly terminated, then a failure to pay settled at the auction final price | 26 | complete, 0.5 s | complete, 1.5 s | complete, 28.1 s |
| Sovereign credit-default swap (`cds_sovereign`) | Euro-area sovereign CDS in EUR, protection sold under a CSA, USD reporting, partly bought back across Christmas, run to maturity. The contract is registered by the step "register-under-agreement" (registry form), once the agreement it names is recorded: an agreement basis needs the agreement first. | 21 | complete, 0.5 s | complete, 1.4 s | complete, 23.2 s |
| Credit-index swap (`cds_index`) | Index CDS bought by Treasury, cleared: a constituent's failure to pay reduces the index factor and the contract goes on; rolled into the new series; terminated. Both series are registered by steps (registry form) once the clearing account they name is recorded. | 30 | complete, 0.6 s | complete, 1.6 s | complete, 32.8 s |
| Credit-basket swap (`credit_basket_swap`) | Bespoke first-to-default basket, physically settled: short first stub, partial termination, the first default settles in full and ends the basket | 16 | complete, 0.3 s | complete, 1.3 s | complete, 19.7 s |
| Credit-index tranche swap (`cds_index_tranche`) | Index tranche 3%-7%, protection sold: a default below the attachment leaves it untouched, the next writes it down by 25%, the third exhausts it | 15 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.2 s | complete, 19.2 s |
| Loan credit-default swap (`loan_cds`) | Bespoke cancellable loan CDS with a long first stub, uncollateralized: premium, partial termination, cancelled on refinancing | 15 | complete, 0.2 s | complete, 1.1 s | complete, 17.7 s |
| Asset-backed credit-default swap (`abs_cds`) | Pay-as-you-go ABS CDS: monthly premium on the outstanding notional; interest shortfall, writedown, reimbursement and amortization recorded by hand; terminated | 20 | complete, 0.4 s | complete, 1.6 s | complete, 24.4 s |

### Ticket, inputs and behaviour

| Product | Ticket and required fields | Automatic inputs | Manual inputs | Settlement and lifecycle events | Accounting and collateral |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cds_single_name` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: reference entity, coupon, dates, premium convention, credit events, settlement after a credit event, product terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Buy protection / Sell protection, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase, termination and roll; the instrument form, Lifecycle events recorded by hand, for credit events and the other events of the reference. Required: Reference entity or index; Running coupon; Maturity; Premium convention; Credit events; Settlement after a credit event; Collateral basis; Trading owner; Action; Notional; State a fill price (the upfront per 100 agreed with the dealer: no executable quote exists) | coupon dates from the IMM roll dates and the USD payment calendar; premium accrued to the step-in date, exchanged at trade; full quarterly coupon on the outstanding notional; settlement date T+3 on the USD calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; independent amount and variation margin from the position-level terms; premium accrued to the credit event date, protection amount, release of collateral when the contract ends | upfront of each trade (stated fill price); marks per 100 entered by hand (dealer); credit event: kind (from the contract's credit events), event date, auction final price, settlement date | Upfront, accrued premium and commission settle T+3 on the USD calendar; coupons in cash on the adjusted coupon date; the credit event settlement on the stated auction settlement date. Open, increase, partial termination at a stated upfront; quarterly full coupon; restructuring refused (not a credit event under these terms); physical settlement refused (auction terms); failure to pay with the auction final price ends the contract; nothing scheduled afterwards; a trade on the ended contract refused | Carried at the mark (upfront per 100 x outstanding notional) less the upfronts paid; accrued premium received at trade carried as owed back until the coupon; coupons, termination results and the credit event settlement are realized P&L; commission expensed; unpriced until a mark is entered. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 1% of notional posted at entry, trued up on increase and termination, returned when the contract ends; variation margin at end of day on the mark, no threshold, 10,000 minimum transfer; collateral received is restricted cash with a liability, returned at the end |
| `cds_sovereign` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: reference entity, coupon, dates, premium convention, credit events, settlement after a credit event, product terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Buy protection / Sell protection, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase, termination and roll; the instrument form, Lifecycle events recorded by hand, for credit events and the other events of the reference; the agreement is recorded first under Treasury, Collateral. Required: Reference entity or index; Running coupon; Maturity; Premium convention; Credit events; Settlement after a credit event; Collateral basis; Trading owner; Action; Notional; State a fill price (the upfront per 100 agreed with the dealer: no executable quote exists) | coupon dates from the IMM roll dates and the TARGET calendar; premium accrued to the step-in date, exchanged at trade; full quarterly coupons; settlement date T+3 on the TARGET calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; variation margin from the agreement; EUR/USD rate (FX fixture) for the reporting-currency figures; maturity with collateral returned | the agreement (counterparty, minimum transfer, currency); upfront of each trade (stated fill price; negative: received by the buyer); marks per 100 entered by hand; a credit event would be recorded by hand (a bankruptcy is refused: not a sovereign credit event) | Upfront, accrued premium and commission settle T+3 on the TARGET calendar (across 25 December); coupons on the adjusted coupon dates. Sell protection, full coupons received, partial buy-back across the Christmas holiday, bankruptcy refused (sovereign terms), coupons through Easter, maturity at 20 June 2027 (paid Monday 21 June); nothing scheduled afterwards | EUR contract in a USD Book: P&L entries at the rate of the day, unrealized at the current rate, the difference the FX effect; carried at the mark; the accrued premium paid at trade carried as accrued income until the coupon; the cost written off at maturity. Collateral: Agreement "CSA Dealer M": variation margin in EUR cash on the mark, no threshold, 25,000 EUR minimum transfer, no independent amount; received collateral restricted and owed back; returned at maturity |
| `cds_index` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: reference entity, coupon, dates, premium convention, credit events, settlement after a credit event, product terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Buy protection / Sell protection, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase, termination and roll; the instrument form, Lifecycle events recorded by hand, for credit events and the other events of the reference; the clearing account is recorded first under Treasury, Collateral; Trading owner Treasury; the strategy instance's Roll for the new series. Required: Reference entity or index; Running coupon; Maturity; Premium convention; Credit events; Settlement after a credit event; Collateral basis; Trading owner; Action; Notional; State a fill price (the upfront per 100 agreed with the dealer: no executable quote exists) | coupon dates from the IMM roll dates and the USD calendar; premium accrued to the step-in date, exchanged at trade; full coupons on the notional outstanding (index factor); upfront on the notional outstanding; settlement date T+3 on the USD calendar; commission from the Book fee schedule; initial margin and variation margin from the clearing terms; the roll package: old series terminated, the notional outstanding entered in the new series | the clearing account (initial margin, currency); upfront of each trade (stated fill price) and the roll at the marks entered by hand; marks per 100 entered by hand for each series; a constituent's credit event: kind, event date, auction final price, weight in the index, settlement date | Upfronts, accrued premium and commission settle T+3 on the USD calendar; coupons on the adjusted coupon dates; the constituent's auction settlement on its stated date. Buy protection; a constituent's failure to pay settled at the auction final price on its weight, the index factor falls to 0.992 and the contract goes on with smaller coupons; roll into Series 47 (one package: terminate Series 46, enter Series 47); terminate Series 47; nothing scheduled afterwards | Carried at the mark on the notional outstanding less the upfronts paid; the defaulted part's cost and carried accrued realized with its settlement; accrued premium received at trade carried until the coupon; coupons, the roll and the termination are realized P&L; commission expensed. Collateral: Cleared account "Meridian Clearing House" posted by Treasury: initial margin 2% of the notional outstanding (trued up when the index factor falls, moved from the old to the new series in the roll, returned at the end); variation margin on the mark each end of day, no minimum transfer, received collateral restricted |
| `credit_basket_swap` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: reference entity, coupon, dates, premium convention, credit events, settlement after a credit event, product terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Buy protection / Sell protection, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase, termination and roll; the instrument form, Lifecycle events recorded by hand, for credit events and the other events of the reference. Required: Reference entity or index; Running coupon; Maturity; Premium convention; Credit events; Settlement after a credit event; Collateral basis; Trading owner; Action; Notional; State a fill price (the upfront per 100 agreed with the dealer: no executable quote exists); Basket reference entities; Protection on default number; Deliverable obligation (physical settlement) | premium schedule from the effective date to the IMM roll dates (short first stub) on the USD payment calendar; premium from the effective date for a trade dealt the day before; settlement date T+3; commission from the Book fee schedule; fixed independent amount from the position-level terms; count of defaults against the trigger; on the trigger, protection on the whole notional and the end of the contract | the basket names and the trigger (first default); upfront and the all-in termination amount (stated fill prices); marks per 100 entered by hand; credit event: the defaulted name (chosen from the basket), kind, date, final price, settlement date | Upfront and termination amounts T+3 on the USD calendar; quarterly premium on the next business day after each roll date; physical settlement of the trigger simulated on paper on the stated date. Buy protection on the basket; first period a short stub; partial termination at an all-in amount; a name outside the basket refused; the first default (bankruptcy) settles physically on the whole notional and ends the basket; a second default refused | Carried at the mark less the upfront; premiums and the settlement realized; the physical settlement shown as par received for the obligations delivered and the obligations taken at the final price (simulated: no bond position is created or delivered). Collateral: Position-level terms: a fixed independent amount of 250,000 posted at entry, unchanged by the partial termination, returned when the basket ends; no variation margin |
| `cds_index_tranche` | Registry form (Instruments, New instrument) for the contract: reference entity, coupon, dates, premium convention, credit events, settlement after a credit event, product terms and the required Collateral terms group; then the instrument drawer, Trade tab (Buy protection / Sell protection, Notional, State a fill price); the strategy instance for increase, termination and roll; the instrument form, Lifecycle events recorded by hand, for credit events and the other events of the reference. Required: Reference entity or index; Running coupon; Maturity; Premium convention; Credit events; Settlement after a credit event; Collateral basis; Trading owner; Action; Notional; State a fill price (the upfront per 100 agreed with the dealer: no executable quote exists); Attachment point; Detachment point | coupon dates and full coupons on the tranche notional outstanding; premium accrued to the step-in date, exchanged at trade; index loss, the tranche writedown between the points, the tranche factor and its end when exhausted; premium accrued on the written-down part to the event date; settlement date T+3; commission; independent amount trued up on the outstanding notional | attachment and detachment points; upfront per 100 (stated fill price); marks per 100 of the outstanding notional entered by hand; each constituent credit event: kind, date, final price, weight in the index | Upfront, accrued and commission T+3 on the USD calendar; coupons on the adjusted coupon dates; writedowns settled in cash on the day they are recorded. Sell protection on the tranche; a default whose loss stays below the attachment point changes nothing; a default inside the tranche writes it down by 25% (factor 0.75, smaller coupons); the default that takes the index loss to the detachment point exhausts the tranche and ends it | Carried at the mark per 100 of the notional outstanding less the upfront received; writedowns paid and the cost share of the written-down part realized; coupons realized; accrued premium paid at trade carried as accrued income until the coupon. Collateral: Position-level terms: independent amount 10% of the tranche notional outstanding, posted at entry, a quarter returned with the writedown, the rest when the tranche is exhausted; no variation margin |

Buy protection; long first stub; partial termination; cancellation on refinancing recorded by hand ends the contract with no protection payment; a credit event afterwards refused | Carried at the mark less the upfront; premiums realized; on cancellation the accrued premium is paid and the cost left is realized; commission expensed. ## System behaviour

The failure, recovery and isolation suite (`test/system`, README there): refusals and blocks, execution under stress, no duplicates, history that survives, Book isolation, hedge reconnection. Test ids `SE:`, `SA:`, `SB:<area>:<case>`; statuses come only from `system-engine.json`, `system-api.json` and `system-browser.json`.

59 cases. Each declares, at each level, the test that runs there or the precise reason it does not; the runners refuse to start when a level has neither, when a reason names a test that does not exist or does not run at that level, or when a case that runs at engine or API level names no browser test covering what the user sees of it (test/system/lib/mapping.mjs).

| Level | Cases | Run at this level | Not applicable (reason given) | Passed | Failed | Not run | Results as of |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Engine | 59 | 41 | 18 | 41 | 0 | 0 | 2026-10-14T15:54:27.886Z |
| API | 59 | 48 | 11 | 48 | 0 | 0 | 2026-10-14T16:00:49.446Z |
| Browser | 59 | 39 | 20 | 39 | 0 | 0 | 2026-10-14T17:43:55.622Z |

Each row adds up: run + not applicable = 59 at every level; passed + failed + not run = run. 20 of the 48 API cases have no browser run of their own; each names the browser test(s) that cover what the user sees of it (the Browser column below). 11 browser cases have no API run (a click, a dialog or a screen is a browser matter; the routes behind them are covered by the tests their API reason names).

### The owner's list at browser level

Each item is triggered from the interface by these browser tests (the list is data in test/system/lib/mapping.mjs and every id is checked to run in the browser). Every browser test ends with the integrity read of every Book clean: nothing (fills, settlements, payments, accruals, orders, strategies, hedge requests, holds, independent-amount collateral) recorded twice.
